*Brian Nlong Zhao,Zhuoyang Pan,James M. Rehg,Jiajun Wu,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Kirin框架，从野外动物视频中重建3D运动，学习大规模运动先验，并生成可用于动画的真实动物运动。还构建了首个大规模视频-文本-运动三元组数据集AiM3D，并开发了多模态（文本+图像）驱动的运动生成方法。最终实现了自动绑定3D模型和动画，推动动物动画自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物运动建模对行为学、动画等领域至关重要，但与人类运动相比，受限于高质量运动数据匮乏。因此，需要新方法大规模获取、处理和生成动物真实运动数据。

Method: 1）利用野外动物视频自动重建3D运动序列；2）配合文本生成AiM3D大规模视频-文本-运动数据集；3）提出视觉引导的运动生成模型，结合文本和图像作为输入，实现多动物运动合成；4）通过现成的图像转3D模型技术，实现自动蒙皮和动画。

Result: 成功构建了首个大规模动物视频-文本-3D运动三元组数据集AiM3D；用所提框架自动生成了各类真实感动物运动，并能直接用于3D资产动画制作，实现了高度自动化的工作流。

Conclusion: Kirin框架及AiM3D数据集为动物运动的建模、分析和动画生成提供了新的基础设施，对学术研究与动画等产业应用具有推动作用。

Abstract: Understanding animal motion is fundamental to modeling animal behavior and biomechanics, yet progress in this area lags far behind human motion research due to the scarcity of high-quality motion data. While human motion can be captured in controlled environments, it is impractical for most animal species, resulting in small, domain-limited datasets that restrict downstream applications such as animation. To address this challenge, we introduce Kirin, a framework that reconstructs motion from video, learns motion priors at scale, and generates realistic motion that can be directly applied to animated assets. Using large collections of in-the-wild animal videos, we reconstruct 3D motion sequences and pair them with captions to create AiM3D, the first large-scale dataset offering aligned video-text-motion tuples for quadruped animals. Building on this dataset, we develop a visual-guided motion generation model that conditions on both text and image to guide the generation of realistic motion across diverse animal species. Finally, by leveraging an off-the-shelf image-to-3D model, we automatically rig and animate 3D meshes using generated motion, producing ready-to-render animated animals. Together, our dataset and framework establish a new foundation for large-scale, text and image conditioned animal motion generation and animation. Project page: https://kirin-ani.github.io/.

</details>


### [20] [SliceBridge: context-consistent repair of corrupted slice intervals in T1-weighted MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01827)
*Jiheng Li,Michael E. Kim,Trent Schwartz,Gaurav Rudravaram,Derek B. Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative,Lianrui Zuo,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SliceBridge方法，专为修复T1加权结构MRI中受损连续切片区间而设计，从而避免因局部损坏导致的整个图像舍弃。该方法利用周围未受损切片的结构信息，重建受损切片，提高了修复区间的一致性和下游分析的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑部结构MRI在采集过程中常出现局部连续切片损坏，这些损坏虽影响分析结果，但若舍弃或重拍会增加成本和操作难度，因此亟需有效修复方法，以最大化数据利用率并减少下游分析偏差。

Method: 作者提出了SliceBridge框架，基于rectified flow matching的方法，对输入的损坏区间切片，结合其前后的完整切片和位置关系进行条件生成。通过对区间切片注入相关噪声、采用共享采样时间及同步采样，促进区间内切片间一致性，修复后直接替换原异常切片。

Result: 在9877例T1加权脑MRI数据上训练测试，在外部581名病例和多种控制腐坏场景下，SliceBridge在修复区间内的slice-to-slice变化误差降低32.9%-41.3%，各区间长度下结构相似性（SSIM）更优，下游分割体积误差由1.95%降至1.05%。

Conclusion: SliceBridge能有效修复MRI中的局部切片损坏，比独立重建方法更好维护切片间结构一致性，提高了后续图像分析的准确性，为实际应用中MRI数据清洗和修复提供了高效工具。

Abstract: Structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) images are sometimes corrupted over a contiguous set of slices, where acquisition, motion, hardware, or reconstruction effects leave a single slice or short interval inconsistent with its neighbors while the rest of the image remains usable. Such localized corruption can bias downstream morphometric analysis, yet discarding or reacquiring an otherwise usable image is costly. We formulate this as an image restoration problem: given the location of the affected interval, reconstruct those slices from the surrounding anatomical and imaging context. We propose SliceBridge, a framework for restoring corrupted slice intervals in T1-weighted MRI using rectified flow matching conditioned on the surrounding intact slices and their relative slice positions. Through-plane consistency is encouraged by coupling the slices within the interval through interval-correlated initial noise, a shared flow time, and synchronized sampling. The restored interval is then inserted back, leaving all other slices unchanged. We trained and validated the model on 9,877 T1-weighted brain MRI volumes from four datasets and evaluated it on 581 external subjects using clean interval withholding and controlled corruptions. Compared with a matched model that reconstructed target slices independently, SliceBridge reduced error in slice-to-slice changes within repaired intervals by 32.9%-41.3% across interval lengths and achieved higher SSIM at every interval length. In controlled-corruption cases, SliceBridge reduced the median error in regional brain volume estimates produced by a downstream segmentation model from 1.95% in corrupted volumes to 1.05%.

</details>


### [21] [Cross-Model Distillation of a Human-Pose Foundation Model from Unannotated Infant Video for Markerless 3D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01840)
*R. James Cotton,Divya Joshi,Colleen Peyton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种利用跨模型蒸馏提升婴儿无标记视频姿态估计的方法，将Sapiens 2模型的优势迁移到SAM 3D Body模型上，从而提升后者对婴儿动作关键点检测和三维姿态恢复的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前婴儿自发动作评估依赖人工评分，过程繁琐、专业性强且存在主观差异，自动化无标记视频评估需求迫切。然而现有姿态识别模型主要针对成年人训练，在婴儿中的效果受限，不同模型在2D关键点和3D姿态恢复上各有千秋，缺乏统一优异的解决方案。

Method: 利用“教师-学生”跨模型蒸馏范式，将2D关键点表现优异的Sapiens 2模型作为教师，给SAM 3D Body模型提供伪标签训练信号，仅用未标注的婴儿视频，通过可微渲染器确保输出3D网格和伪标签一致性，进而微调SAM 3D Body模型。

Result: 在11名婴儿（18次实验、173段多视角视频）进行验证，微调后的SAM 3D Body模型在2D关键点检测准确率（体0.22→0.42，脸0.22→0.42）和3D关节平均误差（25.5→22.2mm）上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 跨模型蒸馏方法有效提升了婴儿无标记视频3D姿态估计的表现，为自动化婴儿神经运动健康评估提供了更可靠技术基础。

Abstract: Spontaneous movement is one of the earliest windows onto an infant's neuromotor health, and structured clinical instruments that score it are validated early predictors of cerebral-palsy risk. However, they require specially trained raters, are time-consuming, and carry inter-rater variability. This motivates automated, video-based markerless assessment, especially as marker-based motion capture is impractical in infants. Yet the foundation models that make markerless capture possible are trained almost entirely on adults: our recent multi-view infant study found that no single model is jointly best, with strong 2D keypoint accuracy and direct 3D body recovery split across different models. While that study identifies this trade-off, it does not resolve it. Here, we perform cross-model distillation from the Sapiens 2 pose model into the SAM 3D Body model, using unannotated infant video alone. A frozen teacher supplies dense pseudo-labels, and a differentiable renderer aligns the predicted mesh to them in the training loop. On eleven held-out infants (18 sessions, 173 recordings) under our prior study's multi-view protocol, fine-tuning improves same-view 2D keypoint agreement with the Sapiens reference (median body percentage of correct keypoints @ 10px 0.22 -> 0.42, face 0.22 -> 0.42) and Procrustes-aligned mean per joint 3D position error (25.5 -> 22.2 mm). This demonstrates how cross-model distillation improves SAM 3D Body model performance on infants.

</details>


### [22] [RAFT-DVC: Resolution-Aware Machine Learning-Based Digital Volume Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01876)
*Zixiang Tong,Lehu Bu,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RAFT的分辨率感知DVC深度学习模型（RAFT-DVC），能够对体数据进行三维全场位移测量，并系统分析了模型分辨率对精度及适用范围的影响，实现了高效、准确且具有良好泛化能力的DVC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数字体相关（DVC）技术能够实现三维全场位移测量，但深度学习DVC模型的内部分辨率对精度和适用范围的具体影响尚不清楚，因此有必要系统研究和优化深度学习DVC系统以提升其实用性和可靠性。

Method: 研究提出三种分辨率（下采样因子s=2,4,8）的RAFT-DVC模型，通过对合成数据和实际体数据（如共聚焦和微CT影像）进行测试，对比不同分辨率下模型的测量精度、适用场景与传统DVC方法的性能，并自定义采样器以校正三维采样一致性问题。

Result: 三种RAFT-DVC模型在不同分辨率下均可定位到约0.017特征网格体素，实现了分辨率与测量误差的经验性线性关系；在不同纹理与变形幅度条件下，与调优的经典DVC方法表现相近或优于传统方法，且具有良好的纹理泛化能力。

Conclusion: RAFT-DVC框架可为三维DVC任务提供高效、分辨率感知、具备定量精度与适用范围刻画的深度网络方案，经过修正的采样器显著提升了网络在不同体数据上的准确性及泛化能力。

Abstract: Digital volume correlation (DVC) provides three-dimensional full-field displacement measurements from volumetric images, but how the internal resolution of a machine-learning-based DVC model affects accuracy and operating range remains poorly understood. Here, we present RAFT-DVC, a resolution-aware family of recurrent all-pairs field transforms (RAFT)-based DVC solvers with encoder downsampling factors s = 2, 4, and 8. Using a matched design, we find that the three solvers localize displacement to approximately 0.017 feature-grid voxel, giving an empirical raw-volume error scaling of approximately 0.017s voxel. The solvers exhibit complementary operating regimes governed jointly by displacement reach and volumetric-texture compatibility. Synthetic benchmarks show that RAFT-DVC achieves errors of the same order as tuned classical DVC under fine-texture, small-to-moderate-displacement conditions and becomes competitive or advantageous under coarse-texture, large-displacement conditions. Frequency-swept tests quantify deformation spatial resolution, while tiled inference enables dense estimation on large volumes. Evaluation on confocal volumetric images acquired during indentation illustrates the importance of matching solver operating regime to deformation magnitude and image texture. Tests on micro-CT images of elastomeric foam, despite training only on particle-labeled synthetic data, provide evidence of cross-texture transfer. We also identify coordinate-order inconsistencies in three-dimensional RAFT correlation sampling and introduce a non-cubic impulse test to verify sampler geometry independently of network training. Correcting the sampler improves native-input accuracy and generalization to unseen volume dimensions. Together, these results establish RAFT-DVC as a fast, resolution-aware framework for dense DVC with characterized accuracy and operating regimes.

</details>


### [23] [SignMatch: Matching Dictionary Signs to Continuous Sign Language Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01886)
*Ryan Wong,Youngjoon Jang,Liliane Momeni,Gül Varol,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将词典手语视频与连续手语视频中的相应手语片段进行匹配的方法，仅依赖于手的形状与动作的视觉相似性。方法在多个数据集和手语语种上表现优异，具有很好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中，手语有大量词典样本和实际连续视频，需要将词典单词与实际交流中的手语进行自动匹配，以辅助识别、检索和标注提效。

Method: 作者提出学习一个原型结构的手语嵌入空间：每个原型代表一个手语类别，该空间通过带标注的连续手语视频进行训练。然后将独立的词典视频映射到该空间，通过嵌入间的相似性进行匹配、检索及扩展到未见手语。

Result: 在ASL-Citizen、ChaLearn OSLWL和BOBSL等多数据集上，方法无需特定监督即可有效迁移，并在美式、英式、西班牙手语上均优于以往方法。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了跨数据集、跨手语语种的高效手语匹配和表达迁移，支持词典驱动的新手语识别与自动标注任务，对手语智能处理有重要意义。

Abstract: The objective of this paper is to match dictionary sign videos to corresponding signs in continuous signing videos, where a match is defined by the visual similarity alone - the handshape and motion relative to the body. To achieve this, we learn a prototype-structured sign embedding space from continuous video annotated with signs, where each learnable prototype corresponds to a sign class. Isolated dictionary videos are then mapped into this sign space, enabling the matching between dictionary exemplars and continuous sign instances. This design supports direct dictionary-guided sign matching through embedding similarity and naturally extends to unseen signs using only dictionary exemplars. Experiments on ASL-Citizen dictionary retrieval, ChaLearn OSLWL dictionary-to-continuous sign matching, and using BOBSL's CSLR2 evaluation for automatic sign annotation demonstrate strong generalisation across datasets, tasks and sign languages. Without benchmark-specific supervision, the learned representation transfers effectively across American, British, and Spanish Sign Languages, outperforming prior methods on all three benchmarks. Project page: https://www.robots.ox.ac.uk/~vgg/research/signmatch/

</details>


### [24] [Does Playing it Safe Count as Faithfulness? Reassessing LVLM Hallucination Mitigation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01888)
*Mehrdad Fazli,Sina Mansouri,Mohit Marvania,Ziwei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了当前LVLMs幻觉缓解方法，在减少幻觉的同时，常以信息量削减为代价，现有评测标准可能夸大了缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然多模态大模型的推理阶段幻觉缓解方法在基准测试中表现提升，但尚不清楚这些提升源自多模态对齐增强还是生成策略过于保守。

Method: 作者在三种LVLM与四个基准上（包括专注幻觉的和能力多样的MMStar）评估了六种缓解幻觉方法。通过对输出详细性、物体召回、视觉覆盖等维度开展深入分析。

Result: 一方面，缓解幻觉常常导致信息量下降（如细节减少、召回率与视觉覆盖降低）；另一方面，在更广泛多模态能力上的提升不稳定，甚至可能退化。

Conclusion: 当前仅依赖幻觉评分的评估方式存在局限，应综合考虑生成的忠实性、信息量与广泛能力，实现三者权衡评估。

Abstract: Recent inference-time hallucination mitigation methods for large vision-language models (LVLMs) report strong gains on hallucination benchmarks. However, it remains unclear whether lower hallucination scores reflect improved multimodal grounding or more conservative generation. We evaluate six mitigation methods across three LVLMs and four benchmarks, including hallucination-focused evaluation and the diverse capability benchmark MMStar. Our analysis reveals two consistent patterns. First, hallucination reduction is often coupled with reduced informativeness: methods that lower hallucination rates also reduce object recall, visual coverage, or response detailedness. Second, improvements on hallucination benchmarks do not reliably transfer to broader multimodal capabilities, with methods showing inconsistent or degraded performance on fine-grained perception and reasoning tasks. Our findings suggest that current evaluation protocols may overestimate progress by rewarding conservative generation. We argue that hallucination mitigation should be evaluated as a faithfulness--informativeness--capability trade-off rather than through hallucination scores alone.

</details>


### [25] [TAPVid-MV: A Benchmark for Tracking Any Point in 3D Across Multiple Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01899)
*Skanda Koppula,Frano Rajic,Abdullah Faiz Ur Rahman,Yi Yang,Ignacio Rocco,Jeet Thakwani,Rishabh Kabra,Andrew Zisserman,Joao Carreira,Siyu Tang,Carl Doersch,Gabriel Brostow*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个跨多视角、动态相机环境下的3D点追踪基准TAPVid-MV，包含丰富多样序列与真实标注，并发现当前方法在该任务上表现均不理想，几乎无解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的点追踪基准主要聚焦于单视频或静态多目环境，未覆盖多视角下且伴随相机运动的长期3D点追踪，这一场景在机器人、AR/VR、自动驾驶等领域极具实际意义。因此，急需有针对性的基准推动该领域研究。

Method: 构建TAPVid-MV数据集，涵盖284段序列、1142路标定相机、109769条点轨迹，场景涉及室内外多类型活动。采用深度传感器、LiDAR、SLAM、SfM点、人体/物体3D网格、仿真等多种辅助信息获取准确追踪，每条轨迹均经人工核查。作者还对30余种多/单视角点追踪基线方法进行了全面评测。

Result: 无论多视角还是单视角方法，当前没有算法能有效解决该基准任务，甚至多视角跟踪器在精度上并不稳定优于单视角。通过联合评估3D重建与点追踪，发现几何恢复效率不足是此类任务的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: TAPVid-MV基准为多视角3D点追踪提供了重要测试平台，同时支持2D/3D单目点追踪、轨迹预测及4D重建等研究。该基准暴露了该领域现有方法的不足，有助于未来方法改进，特别是在几何恢复环节。

Abstract: Multi-camera systems are increasingly practical for robotics, AR/VR, and autonomous driving because complementary views reduce depth ambiguity and preserve visibility under occlusion. Existing point-tracking benchmarks, however, focus on a single video or static multi-camera rigs. None test long-term 3D point tracking across several synchronized views under camera motion. We introduce TAPVid-MV (Tracking Any Point in Video across Multiple Views), the first benchmark for this setting. It contains a curated set of 284 sequences, 1,142 calibrated camera streams, and 109,769 point tracks across seven subsets spanning indoor and outdoor domains, from robotics and human activity to driving and synthetic procedural scenes. We obtain these trajectories using dataset-specific auxiliary modalities: sensor depth, LiDAR, SLAM and SfM points, human meshes, posed object meshes, and simulation. Every sequence and trajectory is visually verified by human annotators.
  Across more than 30 baselines, no method comes close to solving the task. Surprisingly, existing multi-view point trackers do not consistently outperform monocular point trackers. By evaluating reconstruction and point tracking on the same datasets, TAPVid-MV helps distinguish errors in recovered geometry from errors in point correspondence. Through this joint analysis, we identify geometry recovery as a major bottleneck for accurate 3D point tracking. Beyond multi-view 3D point tracking, our released annotations support monocular 2D and 3D point tracking, future-trajectory prediction, and 4D reconstruction.

</details>


### [26] [Automated Maize Ear Phenotyping Using 3D Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01921)
*Ritwesh A. Kumar,Som Tripathi,Peja Matthews,Srikar Reddy,Talukder Zaki Jubery,Patrick Schnable,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一套全自动的玉米穗粒性状三维点云提取与分析流程，大幅提升了玉米粒数、粒行数等性状的测量效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玉米粒相关性状（如行数、每行粒数和粒体积）会影响产量，常与基因组特定区域关联。人工测量效率低，无法满足育种大批量测量需求，因此需开发自动化测量手段。

Method: 基于视频到点云的技术，利用COLMAP与NeRF将玉米视频帧转化为三维点云，通过密度分离、物理单位标定及PCA对齐归一化点云，展开为二维图片后结合Cellpose-SAM进行粒单体分割，通过三重无缝拼接避免计数重复，实现自动化性状提取。

Result: 在268个玉米穗的标注数据集中，168个测试集耳的自动粒数测量R^2为0.921（MAPE=10.33%），粒行数绝对误差为0.75行，95.2%的穗计数误差在正负2行以内。

Conclusion: 该自动化流程实现了高效准确的玉米穗粒性状测量，且能与每个样本已知基因型对应，有助于后续的表型-基因型关联分析。

Abstract: Maize kernel traits such as row number, kernels per row, and kernel size vary largely for genetic reasons and are consistently associated with regions of the genome that influence yield. Manual measurement of these traits, however, cannot keep pace with the volume of maize generated in a breeding program. To address this, we developed and validated a fully automated pipeline for extracting these traits from 3D point clouds of corn ears, built on a recently developed video-to-point-cloud platform. Raw video frames are processed through COLMAP and NeRF, the ear is isolated via density-based separation, and the point cloud is distance-calibrated to physical units. The calibrated ear point cloud was Z-axis aligned via PCA and cylindrically unwrapped to a 2D image. We enhanced contrast and performed zero-fine-tuning instance segmentation using Cellpose-SAM. A triple-juxtaposed unwrap strategy was used to prevent double-counting at the seam. The pipeline achieved kernel count R^2 = 0.921 (MAPE = 10.33%) and kernel row number within +-2 rows for 95.2% of ears (MAE = 0.75 rows) on a 168-ear held-out set from the 268-ear labeled dataset. The resulting multi-trait dataset has known genotype identity for each ear, positioning it for phenotype-to-genotype association analyses.

</details>


### [27] [Learning with Volterra Neural Networks: A System Theoretic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01928)
*Haoyu Yun,Hamid Krim,Yufang Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了kVNN，一种基于可学习核方法的Volterra神经算子，用于高效紧凑地提取高阶交互特征，并在视觉任务中实现了较好的准确率与效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶交互成分对于信号、图像和视频建模很重要，但传统高阶算子通常需要大量参数和计算资源。论文旨在通过核方法提升Volterra类神经算子的效率，并为其高阶成分提供结构化解释。

Method: kVNN结合了Volterra滤波的阶结构与可学习的多项式核原子，每个交互阶由独立可学习的中心和系数表示。这种阶解耦表征避免了显式高阶张量参数化，并能作为兼容CNN的网络层实现。

Result: 实验结果显示，在具有代表性的视觉任务上，kVNN能够实现较好的准确率与效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: kVNN以更紧凑、更高效的方式实现了高阶特征建模，同时为高阶成分提供了结构化解释，展示了其在视觉任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Higher-order interaction components are important for signal, image, and video modeling, but explicit high-order operators often suffer from rapidly increasing parameter and computational costs. This paper presents kVNN, a learnable kernelized Volterra Neural operator for compact higher-order filtering. The motivation is to use kernelization to improve the efficiency of Volterra-type neural operators while providing a structured interpretation of their higher-order components. The proposed formulation combines the order-wise structure of Volterra filtering with learnable polynomial-kernel atoms, allowing different interaction orders to be represented by separate learnable centers and coefficients. This order-decoupled representation avoids explicit high-order tensor parameterization and can be implemented as a CNN-compatible layer. Experiments on representative vision tasks show that kVNN achieves a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [28] [Aggregating Neighbor Embedding Projection and Rank-Based Manifold Learning for Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01963)
*Vinicius Atsushi Sato Kawai,Gustavo Rosseto Leticio,Lucas Pascotti Valem,Daniel Carlos Guimarães Pedronette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合投影与排序流形学习的图像检索框架，通过秩聚合融合UMAP投影和基于排序的重排序方法，有效提升了检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习推动了基于内容的图像检索(CBIR)进步，但高维空间下仅用距离测度难以捕捉上下文关系，视觉特征与语义间存在语义鸿沟。现有流形学习与排序精炼方法虽各有优势，但难以有机融合其互补性。

Method: 提出的框架利用UMAP生成低维特征投影，同时采用基于秩的重排序方法，然后用Borda Count聚合这两类排序结果，从而融合上下文和流形信息。实验用ResNet152、Swin Transformer、DINOv2等特征，在多个公开数据集上验证方法有效性。

Result: 该方法在多种场景下均提升了检索效果，尤其在基线表现不佳时优势明显。秩聚合方法改善了排序前列的检索质量，在不同数据集和特征提取器上取得了竞争性的MAP和精度值。

Conclusion: 结合基于投影和基于排序的流形学习，通过秩聚合可互补模型上下文信息，有效改善图像检索的检索效果。

Abstract: Content-based image retrieval (CBIR) has advanced significantly with deep learning, yet effectively ranking similar images remains challenging, particularly in high-dimensional feature spaces, where pairwise distances often fail to capture contextual relationships and the semantic gap between visual features and high-level concepts persists. Manifold learning and rank-based refinement methods have emerged as complementary strategies, respectively improving feature representations and exploiting contextual information embedded in ranked lists, such as neighborhood relationships among images. However, combining these projection-based and rank-based strategies to exploit their complementary properties remains a challenging research problem. To address this, we propose a framework that combines neighbor embedding projections with rank-based manifold learning through rank aggregation. Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (UMAP) generates alternative low-dimensional feature representations, and ranked lists obtained from UMAP projections and rank-based re-ranking methods are combined using the Borda Count aggregation strategy. Experiments were conducted on several public datasets using deep learning features extracted from ResNet152, Swin Transformer, and DINOv2 models. Results show that the proposed approach improves retrieval effectiveness in several scenarios, particularly when the baseline representation struggles to achieve high precision. The aggregation strategy also often improves the quality of top-ranked positions, leading to competitive Mean Average Precision (MAP) and Precision values across different datasets and feature extractors. These findings suggest that combining projection-based and rank-based manifold learning strategies through rank aggregation can provide complementary contextual information for image retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [29] [Morphology signal in whole slide image foundation models can automatically triage slides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01987)
*Ayushi Sinha,Shashank Yadav,Benjamin Holmes,Pravat Das,Aaron W. Bogan,James S. Lewis,Santiago Romero-Brufau,Andrew Y. K. Foong,Scott H. Kaufmann,Kathryn M. Van Abel,David M. Routman,Michael R. Lucas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用公开可用的WSI基础模型，通过zero-shot分类的方法对患者的多张病理切片进行自动排序，有效筛选含肿瘤信息的切片，减少人工筛选负担。实验结果表明，该方法可准确把含肿瘤的切片排在前列，在多数据集上均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前病理诊断中每例患者常产生多张切片，人工挑选用于建模的关键切片耗时耗力。已有数据集假设每患者仅有1张切片或全部纳入训练，但两者皆存在信号稀释或样本偏差等问题。因此亟需自动、高效筛选关键切片的方法。

Method: 作者提出利用WSI基础模型，结合zero-shot分类预测，对所有切片进行排序。瓶颈在于通过模型无监督方式判断哪些切片包含最多肿瘤或关键信息，并提出了一种新的排序评估方法用于量化模型在切片筛选任务中的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法能够准确将含肿瘤切片排在各患者的前2名（即top-2），即使患者的切片数高达43张。方法在多个公开数据集上均表现出色。

Conclusion: WSI基础模型包含丰富的形态学信息，可被用来自动化预筛病理切片，大幅减轻人工筛选负担，对大规模病理影像AI训练和应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Patient exams in the cancer diagnosis and staging process typically generate several whole slide images (WSIs). One of the initial steps in training models on WSI data is identifying one or a few slides containing tumor or other diagnostic biomarkers necessary for downstream prediction tasks such as estimating recurrence risk or progression-free survival. This step requires tedious manual curation by experienced pathologists. Many published datasets make the artificial assumption of 1 slide per patient. Alternatively, all slides per patient may be used for model training, which may dilute the signal from the few slides containing tumor or other relevant information. In this paper, we present a pipeline to overcome these challenges using publicly available WSI foundation models (FMs). Our evaluations show that ranking WSIs based on predictions from zero-shot classification using WSI FMs accurately identifies slides with the most tumor, indicating that WSI FMs contain sufficient morphology signal to automatically triage slides. We also present a formulation for ranked evaluation to benchmark FM performance in slide triage. We show, on multiple datasets, that tumor slides are identified in the top-2 ranked slides for patients with up to 43 slides.

</details>


### [30] [Linear Fusion MultiDiffusion for Fast Training-Free Spherical Panorama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01997)
*Akio Hayakawa,Yusuke Mukuta,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LF-MultiDiffusion的全景图生成方法，无需额外训练，通过新颖的线性投影和高效求解方式，提升了全景生成的效率和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练自由（training-free）全景生成方法在映射稠密性和自然性方面存在瓶颈，同时推理效率也较低。因此，作者希望提出一种无需额外训练、能高效生成高质量全景图的新方法。

Method: 作者将潜变量聚合问题表述为带正则的最小二乘问题，并在去噪循环里用Krylov迭代求解器高效求解。这样能够实现target和reference图像空间间的线性投影，显著提升映射质量。同时，LF-MultiDiffusion显著减少了去噪过程中图像生成器的调用次数。

Result: 实验显示，LF-MultiDiffusion在视觉质量、文本对齐和全景一致性方面都优于现有最强训练自由基线方法，同时推理速度提升高达15.36倍。

Conclusion: LF-MultiDiffusion在无需额外训练的前提下，实现了更高效和高质量的全景图生成，为全景生成任务提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose LF-MultiDiffusion, a training-free panorama generation method that extends MultiDiffusion to support linear projections between target and reference image spaces. Our key idea is to reformulate latent aggregation as a regularized least-squares problem and solve it efficiently with a Krylov-based iterative solver inside the denoising loop. This formulation enables denser and more natural mappings than prior training-free methods, yielding more stable generation with far fewer perspective views. As a result, LF-MultiDiffusion reduces the number of image generator evaluations during denoising and significantly improves inference efficiency. Experiments show that LF-MultiDiffusion achieves better visual quality, text alignment, and panoramic consistency than the strongest training-free baseline, while providing a 15.36$\times$ speedup. Our project page is available at: https://ahykw.github.io/lfmd.

</details>


### [31] [Who Drives the Probability Game of VLMs? A Temporal Causal Drive Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02000)
*Shuyao Xiao,Shengling Wang,Haoyu Niu,Ke Chao,Changwei Xu,Xinran Duan,Chaoyong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果和时序分析框架，用于深入解析视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在多模态生成过程中的不同信息源影响，并评估了该框架在多个数据集和模型上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前主流的评测指标主要关注视觉-语言模型的最终答案质量，忽略了生成过程中各信息源（视觉输入、问题文本、先前生成内容）的动态作用机制。该工作旨在填补这一评估空白，帮助理解模型生成不同答案时各信息源的因果驱动模式。

Method: 作者基于结构因果模型（Structural Causal Model），通过干预与背门调整，提出了三种因果驱动指标：视觉因果驱动（VCD）、问题因果驱动（QCD）、前缀因果驱动（PCD），并在自回归解码过程中对各信息源的影响进行逐步追踪分析，无需参考答案。主要在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型以及多个数据集和模型上进行了实验和交叉验证。

Result: 实验证明，模型生成过程中存在由题目和视觉引导转向依赖已生成内容的趋势。与观测PMI基线相比，因果驱动指标QCD和PCD能显著降低恢复误差（分别为34.8%和47.1%）。此外，提出的前缀-视觉不平衡分数可有效区分先验驱动和视觉驱动的生成（AUROC为0.767，AUPRC为0.873）。

Conclusion: 因果和时序驱动指标为多模态生成模型提供了细粒度、补充性的源级解释工具，有助于更深入理解和诊断视觉-语言模型的生成行为。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly evaluated on complex image and video understanding tasks, yet conventional metrics primarily assess final-answer quality and reveal little about how different information sources shape the generation process. We propose a causal and temporal evaluation framework that traces the evolving roles of visual input, question text, and generated prefixes during autoregressive decoding. Grounded in a Structural Causal Model, we use interventions and backdoor adjustment to derive three step-indexed causal-drive metrics---Visual Causal Drive (VCD), Question Causal Drive (QCD), and Prefix Causal Drive (PCD)---for characterizing source-specific generation patterns without requiring reference answers. Experiments on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct across MAVIS, LLaVA-Video-178K, and MiraData, together with cross-model validation on InternVL2-8B, reveal a consistent transition from stronger early question and visual guidance toward increasing reliance on generated prefixes. Randomized-intervention validation shows that QCD and PCD reduce recovery error over observational PMI baselines by 34.8\% and 47.1\%, respectively. On VLMBias, the prefix--visual imbalance score achieves 0.767 AUROC and 0.873 AUPRC for distinguishing prior-driven from visually grounded generations. These results show that causal-drive trajectories provide complementary source-level diagnostics for multimodal generation.

</details>


### [32] [InsightSeg: Reusing Correction Insights for Guideline-Consistent Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02002)
*Vanshika Vats,Ashwani Rathee,James Davis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了InsightSeg系统，通过引入情节性记忆机制，将成功的纠错经验转化为可复用的视觉洞察，从而减少重复错误，提高语义分割的质量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体的语义分割系统虽然能纠正符合指南的细粒度错误，但每次修正后的反馈不会被长期利用，导致同类错误反复出现和纠正，增加了计算成本，降低了效率。

Method: InsightSeg系统包含一个情节性记忆机制，每次成功纠错后，通过元分析器将其萃取成基于自然语言的洞察，并锚定到具体的图像区域，再通过与后续图片的视觉特征匹配，将相关经验应用到未来的新样本预测中，防止相同错误的再次出现。

Result: 在Waymo和Cityscapes数据集上，InsightSeg在首轮和最终的分割准确率上都有提升，同时所需的修正次数更少。

Conclusion: 在语义分割任务中，借助以往纠错经验能使多智能体系统更准确、高效。InsightSeg展示了记忆机制在提升符合指南的分割质量和减少无谓修正方面的优势。

Abstract: Guideline-consistent semantic segmentation requires more than category recognition, as real-world labeling policies demand fine-grained, task-specific decisions. Recent multi-agent refinement systems improve compliance with such textual guidelines by detecting and correcting errors. However, they are stateless: feedback from the critiquing agent is discarded, causing the same guideline-specific mistakes to be repeatedly rediscovered and corrected across the dataset at the cost of additional refinement. We introduce InsightSeg, an episodic memory mechanism that converts successful correction episodes into reusable, visually grounded insights. A meta-analyzer distills each qualifying episode into directive natural-language insights and anchors them to the local image regions that caused the error using patch-level visual concept vectors. On subsequent images, these concepts are matched against dense patch embeddings to retrieve relevant insights, which condition the segmenting agent before making its first prediction. This shifts the system from correcting recurring errors to preventing them, improving segmentation quality before any refinement occurs. Across Waymo and Cityscapes, InsightSeg improves both first-pass and final guideline-consistent segmentation performance while requiring fewer refinement steps, demonstrating that multi-agent refinement can become more accurate and efficient by drawing on past correction experience.

</details>


### [33] [InstEditSeg: Instruction-Driven Image Editing for Polyp and Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02004)
*Ziquan Liu,Zhewei Zhu,Xuyang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的医学分割方法InstEditSeg，将医学图像分割重构为基于指令的图像编辑任务，利用预训练生成模型的视觉先验来增强分割性能，并克服现有方法在对比度低、边界模糊、域泛化性差等方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分割方法难以处理低对比度、边界模糊和跨域分布的问题，同时很少充分利用大型生成模型中的视觉先验。作者希望通过引入生成式模型和指令驱动的图像编辑方式，提高分割在不同条件下的准确性和泛化能力。

Method: InstEditSeg将分割视为图像编辑任务，通过文本指令控制模型生成带颜色叠加的输出图像，利用潜在扩散模型对自然/医学图像分布的学习缩小域差异。方法引入DINOv3为辅助视觉编码器，建立多尺度特征金字塔，并通过通道拼接和零初始化卷积与扩散U-Net融合。采用双分支无条件指导策略，提升推理效率。

Result: 在肠息肉和皮肤病变的公开数据集上，InstEditSeg达到了与现有判别式强基线相当的准确率。同时表现出生成式框架的优越性，包括更强的跨域泛化、多病变分割能力、基于指令的任务控制以及采样灵活性。

Conclusion: InstEditSeg有效结合生成模型视觉先验和医学分割任务，提升了泛化能力和多任务适应性，展示了生成式分割新范式的优势，但方法在颜色敏感性和属性控制上仍存不足。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of polyps and skin lesions is pivotal for clinical diagnosis, yet existing methods struggle with low contrast, ambiguous boundaries, and cross-domain distribution discrepancies. Discriminative networks and most diffusion-based segmentation approaches predict standalone binary masks, leaving the visual priors of large-scale pretrained generative models largely unexploited. We propose InstEditSeg, a unified generative framework that reformulates medical segmentation as an instruction-driven image editing problem. Instead of emitting a mask, the model renders a color-coded overlay on the original image, conditioned on a textual instruction, so that the edited output aligns with the natural image distribution learned by latent diffusion models and mitigates the domain gap between natural and medical imagery. To recover fine anatomical structures, we introduce DINOv3 as an auxiliary visual encoder and a DINO Feature Guidance Block that builds a multi-scale feature pyramid. The pyramid is fused into the diffusion U-Net by channel concatenation and zero-initialized convolution so that hierarchical discriminative priors can be injected without perturbing the pretrained weights. A dual-branch classifier-free guidance strategy requiring only two forward passes per denoising step reduces inference cost. On polyp and skin lesion benchmarks the framework achieves accuracy competitive with strong discriminative baselines, and it further demonstrates concrete advantages of the generative formulation: notably better cross-domain generalization on unseen data, more complete multi-lesion segmentation, instruction-conditioned task control, and sampling flexibility. We also analyze the strengths and limitations of the paradigm, including its color sensitivity and unsupported attribute-conditioned selection. Code is available at: https://github.com/wincharm001/InstEditSeg.

</details>


### [34] [GeoStore: Finding Small Storefronts in Large Scenes -- A Fine-Grained POI Localization Benchmark with Global-to-Local Asymmetric Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02012)
*Lu Han,Xiting Sun,Hao Wang,Zhiqiang Cao,Ruihuan Du,Ziquan Zeng,Chunlong Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoStore，这是第一个专注于POI（兴趣点）本地化任务的基准数据集，并提出了新的匹配方法GLAM，有效提升了查准率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉地点识别方法无法有效处理POI本地化这种非对称、细粒度匹配问题，因为POI本地化需要将包含目标店铺特写照的查询与全景街景图片中小区域的目标进行匹配，存在大尺度、位置和视域差异。

Method: 论文建立了新的GeoStore数据集，并提出GLAM（Global-to-Local Asymmetric Matching）方法：首先用全局特征进行检索锚定，然后利用区域特征token和可学习交互进行局部精细匹配。推理时，可高效利用区块token进行高效的互为最近邻重排名。

Result: GLAM算法在Recall@1/5/10和mAP等指标上都优于主流的全局和两阶段基线方法，且重排名阶段的特征体积仅为原方法的1/5，匹配计算量比之前方法低2个数量级。

Conclusion: GLAM 提供了高效、精细且可扩展的POI本地化方案，为地图、POI验证、地理定位等应用带来实用进展。GeoStore有望成为该领域新的重要基准。

Abstract: Point-of-interest (POI) localization -- matching a user's close-up storefront photograph against large-scale geo-tagged street-view imagery -- underpins map construction, POI verification, and location-based services. Its closest existing paradigm, visual place recognition (VPR), assumes symmetric, whole-image matching of the same scene at a comparable scale; POI localization instead must match a close-up query, in which the target fills the frame, against wide references in which the same POI occupies only a small, off-center region among visually similar shops, under a substantial capture-domain gap. We introduce GeoStore, to our knowledge the first benchmark dedicated to this asymmetric, fine-grained, open-set formulation, and show that global-descriptor methods tuned for symmetric VPR are systematically limited on it, since a single global vector dilutes the small target. We further propose GLAM (Global-to-Local Asymmetric Matching), which couples a retrieval-anchoring global descriptor with an asymmetric local pathway: each reference is kept as a compact set of pooled region tokens and matched against a single query probe through a learnable soft late interaction; at inference, the same tokens enable a lightweight mutual-nearest-neighbor re-ranking. GLAM surpasses strong global and two-stage baselines on Recall@1/5/10 and mAP, with ~5x smaller re-ranking features and ~two orders of magnitude lower per-pair matching cost than prior local re-ranking. The benchmark and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [35] [Detecting Object Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models via Cross-Modal Attention Drifts and Mask-Based Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02028)
*Xuanbing Wen,Boxu Chen,Le Yang,Jiakai Wang,Zhengyu Zhao,Chenhao Lin,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖且轻量的目标幻觉检测方法CADMP，通过层间注意力漂移与视觉掩蔽敏感性联合提升检测准确性。实验显示该方法在多项基准测试和开源LVLMs上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模视觉语言模型（LVLMs）发展迅速，但目标幻觉问题依然严重，影响其实际应用。现有检测多利用单层注意力，忽略了跨层注意力演变信息。

Method: 提出CADMP框架，结合相邻层的跨模态注意力漂移及预测对视觉掩蔽的敏感性，通过量化解码过程中注意力分布的突变，并结合预测概率变化进行联合检测。最终用轻量化探测器综合两类信号判断幻觉预测。

Result: 在多个主流基准和典型开源LVLMs上，CADMP检测性能稳定、优异。消融实验也证实了注意力漂移和掩蔽验证两种信号的互补性和有效性。

Conclusion: CADMP有效提升了LVLM目标幻觉检测能力，展现出较强的泛化和实用潜力，对未来LVLM部署提供了可靠的技术支持。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large vision-language models (LVLMs), object hallucination remains a major barrier to their reliable deployment. Existing detection methods often characterize visual grounding using attention from individual layers, leaving its evolution across layers underexplored. We propose CADMP, a lightweight object hallucination detection framework that combines adjacent-layer cross-modal attention drift with prediction sensitivity to targeted visual masking. During decoding, CADMP quantifies distributional changes between consecutive cross-modal attention maps to capture abrupt transitions in visual grounding. It then selects the transition with the largest drift, locates the corresponding visually relevant regions, and measures the change in prediction probability after masking these regions. These two signals provide complementary evidence: attention drift characterizes the stability of internal visual grounding, while probability variation verifies whether a prediction truly depends on the identified visual evidence. A lightweight detector integrates both signals to identify hallucinated predictions. Experiments on multiple benchmarks and representative open-source LVLMs demonstrate that CADMP achieves consistently competitive detection performance. Ablation studies further confirm the complementary contributions of adjacent-layer drift modeling and mask-based grounding verification.

</details>


### [36] [SelfLift: Accelerating Few-Step Diffusion via Self-Recovering Resolution Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02036)
*Tingyan Wen,Chenqian Yan,Xurui Peng,Xiazhang Fang,Shuai Wang,Xueqian Wang,Songwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SelfLift框架，以应对高分辨率扩散模型推理中空间计算成本过高的问题，通过分辨率渐进推理和自恢复机制，在显著降低推理延迟的同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着few-step扩散模型极大压缩了时间计算，单次模型推理的空间计算成本成为延迟瓶颈。已有的渐进式分辨率推理在高分辨率切换时易产生分布失配，导致伪影，且难以在后续步骤修正，尤其在步数很少时问题严重。因此，亟需一种能更高效安全完成低到高分辨率过渡的新方法。

Method: 提出了SelfLift框架，包括两个核心部分：1）SelfLift-zero，无需额外训练，通过直接潜变量提升与像素VAE重编码之间的差异，作为本地伪影风险信号和自动矫正方向，从而安全地进行分辨率切换；2）SelfLift-rich，对学生模型的访问状态施以高分辨率内部自教师的密集指导，实现对分辨率变化后的动态对齐与自恢复。方案无需外部超分、无需增加去噪器评估次数或更改采样日程。

Result: 在FLUX.2-Klein和Z-Image-Turbo等基准上，SelfLift将端到端延迟分别降低了41.5%和44.1%。结合时间步蒸馏技术，总运行速度比50步基线模型提升了29.61倍（FLUX.2-Klein）和19.21倍（Z-Image-Turbo），同时保持有竞争力的生成质量。

Conclusion: SelfLift为few-step扩散模型树立了新的速度-质量最优前沿。它无需外部资源，有效解决了分辨率切换产生的分布失配问题，大幅提升推理效率，有望在实际部署中广泛应用。

Abstract: Few-step diffusion models substantially compress temporal computation, making the spatial cost of each model evaluation an increasingly dominant source of inference latency. Progressive-resolution inference reduces this cost by performing early denoising at low resolution and reserving high-resolution computation for refinement. However, existing methods typically lift intermediate latents directly and rely on subsequent steps to absorb the induced distribution mismatch. In the few-step regime, the limited recovery budget leaves these errors as visible artifacts, constraining how late the transition can occur and, consequently, how efficiently it can be performed. We introduce SelfLift, a self-recovering progressive-resolution framework that derives both transition-repair signals and trajectory-aligned supervision from the generative model itself. SelfLift-zero proposes a training-free Artifact-Aware Consistency Lift, using disagreement between direct latent lifting and pixel-VAE re-encoding as both a localized artifact-risk signal and a model-native correction direction. It enables reliable late transitions without external super-resolution, extra denoiser evaluations, or sampling-schedule modifications. Building on this robust transition, SelfLift-rich performs On-Policy Self Recovery on student-visited states, transferring dense high-resolution guidance from an internal self-teacher while remaining aligned with the altered progressive-resolution dynamics. Across FLUX.2-Klein and Z-Image-Turbo, SelfLift reduces end-to-end latency by 41.5% and 44.1%, respectively. Combined with timestep distillation, it delivers overall speedups of 29.61x and 19.21x over the corresponding 50-step models while preserving competitive generation quality, establishing a stronger speed-quality frontier for few-step diffusion.

</details>


### [37] [LookStep: Efficient Vision-Language Navigation with Linguistic Foresight and Event Driven Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02350)
*Kun-Yang Yu,Yingzhe Li,Hongyu Xu,Shi-Yu Tian,Zhi Zhou,Yang Chen,Ming Yang,Sheng Wang,Qing Yu,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LookStep框架，实现了资源高效的视觉-语言导航（VLN），减少了内存和数据需求，同时提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往方法对环境和状态维护依赖较重，且对数据需求量大，导致资源消耗高，限制了实际应用。

Method: LookStep是一个端到端框架，结合了基于语言的未来状态建模和事件驱动的滚动记忆。它用语言标签生成粗粒度导航进度和候选动作的未来状态，并根据语义角色自主决定是否将当前观察写入有限的记忆中。

Result: 在VLN-CE任务中，LookStep在使用相同训练条件下取得了49.7%的R2R-CE Val-Unseen任务成功率，优于现有方法，同时提升了内存效率并减少了数据用量。

Conclusion: LookStep有效降低了VLN任务的资源消耗，并提升了性能，为实际部署带来了可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to follow natural-language instructions in unseen environments. Recent progress has been largely driven by Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Existing methods follow a next-step action prediction paradigm, supervising only the expert action, which requires a high quantity of data for training. They also rely on cognitive maps, accumulated historical frames, or external 3D tools to maintain states, leading to high computational and memory overhead. To realize resource efficiency VLN, we propose LookStep, a unified end-to-end framework that combines Language Centric Future State Modeling and Event Driven Rolling Memory that uses language labels to generate coarse-grained navigation progress and future states for each candidate action, while autonomously deciding whether to write each observation into a bounded rolling memory with a semantic role. We validate LookStep empirically. On VLN-CE tasks, LookStep outperforms existing methods under the same training settings, achieving a 49.7\% success rate on R2R-CE Val-Unseen with better memory efficiency and less data usage. Code and model is available at https://github.com/kunyang-YU/LookStep.

</details>


### [38] [Test-Time Logit Prompting for Source-Free Missing Modality Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02039)
*Taixi Chen,Nancy Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Test-Time Logit Prompting (TLP)的无源轻量级测试时自适应方法，用于应对视觉-语言模型在实际部署中常见的模态缺失问题，有效提升视觉识别表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言模型在缺失模态输入（如缺图像或文本）时性能显著下滑。现有提升鲁棒性的方法严重依赖原始训练数据，而这些数据常因隐私、存储或可访问性原因不可用，尤其在临床和个性化AI应用中尤为突出。因此，需要探索在无源情况下适应缺失模态输入的新方法。

Method: 提出了一种测试时轻量级适应框架Test-Time Logit Prompting (TLP)，通过优化logit提示、引入不确定性感知调整和模态完备一致性约束，实现在测试时自适应调整预测置信度并保持语义一致性，无需访问源训练数据。

Result: 在多个视觉-语言基准上进行了广泛实验，TLP在模态缺失场景下平均性能提升高达8%，且仅需优化数百个参数及少量测试时迭代步数，验证了方法的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: TLP证明了无需源数据即可在测试阶段高效适应模态缺失，显著增强了视觉-语言模型在实际部署中的适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved remarkable performance by leveraging complementary information from large-scale image-text pairs. However, missing-modality inputs are commonly encountered during real-world deployment, often leading to significant performance degradation. Existing methods primarily enhance model robustness by learning modality compensation strategies from source training data. However, their reliance on source training data makes them difficult to apply when original data are unavailable due to privacy, storage, or accessibility constraints, such as clinical applications and personalized AI services. This raises an important yet underexplored question: can VLMs be efficiently adapted at test time for visual recognition with missing modalities without accessing source training data? To this end, we propose Test-Time Logit Prompting (TLP), a lightweight source-free test-time adaptation framework for visual recognition with missing modalities. To address missing-induced prediction shifts, TLP optimizes logit prompts with uncertainty-aware adjustment and modality-complete consistency regularization, adaptively adjusting prediction confidence while preserving semantic consistency. Extensive experiments across diverse vision-language benchmarks demonstrate that TLP consistently enhances recognition performance under missing-modality scenarios, achieving up to 8\% improvements while requiring only hundreds of tunable parameters and a few test-time optimization steps.

</details>


### [39] [Spatially Aware World Action Model via Geometric Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02531)
*Javier Alejandro Lopetegui Gonzalez,Paul Pacaud,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种空间感知世界动作模型（SA-WAM），首次将RGB与深度信息一起引入到世界动作建模中，提升了机器人在复杂环境中的预测与决策能力，并在多个基准测试中取得了领先效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的世界动作模型（WAMs）虽然能从大规模视频中学习丰富的视觉与物理先验，但大多仅基于RGB数据，忽略了有价值的3D信息。因此，需要一种能够同时利用RGB和3D深度信息的模型来提升机器人的场景理解与任务表现。

Method: SA-WAM利用大规模预训练视频扩散模型，通过非线性编码方法将原始深度信号映射到VAE分词器可接受的范围，实现了RGB和深度的联合建模，不需要专门针对3D进行再训练。模型在不丢失预训练先验的前提下整合几何信息，用于同步预测未来场景的视觉观测和机器人动作。

Result: SA-WAM在RoboCasa与LIBERO-Plus两个基准上实现了最新最好性能，并在真实机器人（UR5机械臂）实验及随机化环境中明显优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SA-WAM有效融合了RGB与深度信息，提升了世界建模和动作预测能力，实现了更强的泛化与未来状态预测表现，为基于视频的机器人策略学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage the capabilities of large-scale pretrained video diffusion models to jointly predict future observations and actions, inheriting rich visual and physical priors from internet-scale video. This has made them a promising paradigm for robot policy learning, yet the prevailing models operate exclusively on RGB observations and do not leverage 3D information. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Spatially Aware World Action Model (SA-WAM), which repurposes a pretrained video model for joint action, RGB, and depth prediction, enabling 3D-aware world modeling and action prediction within a single diffusion backbone. We use a nonlinear encoding that maps the unbounded depth signal into the bounded input domain expected by the frozen VAE tokenizer. This allows us to reuse the tokenizer without 3D-specific fine-tuning, incorporating geometric information without sacrificing the pretrained priors. SA-WAM achieves state-of-the-art results on the RoboCasa and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks, while simultaneously improving future-state predictions. Furthermore, SA-WAM outperforms strong baselines in real-world evaluation using a UR5 robotic arm, with strong gains in randomized environments. We analyze the correlation between world model prediction quality and rollout success, providing insights into WAM performance and avenues for its improvement.

</details>


### [40] [LaST-SR: Laplace-Inspired Steady-Transient Complex-Frequency Decomposition for Single Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02063)
*Linhao Li,Zhaojie Pan,Langkun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为LaST-SR的新颖单幅图像超分辨率方法，有效融合全局傅里叶与局部复频信息，实现了结构一致性和细节恢复的提升，在多个基准测试上效果领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超分方法利用傅里叶算子进行全局建模，但傅里叶本征的周期性限制了其对局部非周期结构与细节的恢复。因此，需要一种同时兼顾全局和局部非周期特征表达的方法。

Method: 作者借鉴动力系统中的拉普拉斯神经算子，首次将稳态-瞬态分解理论扩展到二维特征图以建模周期与非周期信息。方法主干为LaST-SR模型，包括：1）复频分解模块，耦合全局傅里叶分支和基于窗口的局部复频分支；2）稳态-瞬态协同聚合模块，实现双分支特征交互和融合。

Result: 在五个公开SISR基准上，LaST-SR在2倍和4倍超分场景下，PSNR与SSIM都优于已有方法。消融实验也证明了其架构和关键子模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用复频分解与协同聚合，LaST-SR有效提升了单图超分的结构恢复和细节表现，是全球-局部协同建模的有效新范式。

Abstract: Single-image super-resolution (SISR) requires global context modeling for structurally consistent reconstruction. Fourier operators are increasingly adopted for global feature modeling. However, their periodic spectral bases constrain the representation of localized aperiodic variations, limiting the recovery of irregular structures and fine details. In dynamical systems, the Laplace neural operator extends Fourier modes to complex frequencies and decomposes the output signal into complementary steady-state and transient responses to jointly model periodic and aperiodic information. We derive, for the first time, an approximate steady-transient decomposition for two-dimensional feature maps, providing an analytical basis for the proposed complex-frequency decomposition. Accordingly, we propose LaST-SR, centered on a Complex-Frequency Decomposition module that couples a global full-spectrum Fourier branch for image-wide dependencies and long-range structural consistency with a window-conditioned local complex-frequency branch for localized, content-dependent aperiodic variations. To fuse the resulting features, we further design a Steady-Transient Collaborative Aggregation module for cross-branch interaction and joint aggregation. Experiments on five benchmarks show that LaST-SR achieves the best PSNR/SSIM among the compared methods for $\times2$ and $\times4$ SISR. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed architecture and its key modeling mechanisms.

</details>


### [41] [MV-dVRK: A Multi-Viewpoint Benchmark for Spatial Surgical Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02717)
*Guido Caccianiga,Sergey Prokudin,Yutong Chen,Bernard Javot,Rachael L'Orsa,Omer Burak Aladağ,Yarden Sharon,Jens Rolinger,Ivan Capobianco,Anton Deguet,Siyu Tang,Katherine J. Kuchenbecker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并公开了MV-dVRK外科多视角数据集，并系统评测了多种3D重建方法在实际内窥镜场景下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前临床遥控机器人通常仅用单一立体摄像头，真实多视角内窥镜图像极为稀缺，导致多视角3D重建方法缺乏真实外科数据的系统评估。

Method: 作者构建了MV-dVRK数据集，融合多个同步曝光的立体视角、精确表面几何与相机位姿，涵盖静态与动态子集。以此数据集，系统评测零样本单目、立体、多立体、多视角等3D重建方法随视角数量变化的表现。

Result: 使用两个内窥镜时，多立体重建法覆盖范围最广；引入第三视角时，优化型多视角方法表现最佳，67%表面点在1mm误差内，位姿也更精确。而端到端基础模型同场景仅覆盖43%。

Conclusion: MV-dVRK为真实内窥镜多视角3D感知研究奠定了数据基础，并揭示优化型多视角方法在复杂外科环境下的重建优势。该数据集可助力多视角外科感知的进一步发展。

Abstract: Large-scale training and refined optimization techniques have greatly improved sparse multi-view 3D reconstruction. Despite their relevance to surgery, such methods have never before been rigorously evaluated on real endoscopic images. Current clinical telerobots deploy a single stereo camera inside the patient, making multi-viewpoint data extremely rare. This paper presents MV-dVRK, the first ex-vivo surgical dataset to combine multiple exposure-synchronized stereo viewpoints with accurate surface geometry and camera poses. The static subset of the benchmark provides dense SfM reference geometry, validated against an industrial 3D scanner, together with ground-truth camera poses and sparse-view test sets. We use MV-dVRK to systematically compare zero-shot monocular, stereo, multi-stereo, and multi-view 3D reconstruction methods as the number of viewpoints increases. With two endoscopes, multi-stereo reconstruction achieves the highest coverage. With a third viewpoint, optimization-based multi-view methods perform best, covering 67% of ground-truth surface points within a 1 mm tolerance and recovering highly accurate relative camera poses. By contrast, feed-forward foundation models cover only 43% of the ground-truth surface in the same setting. MV-dVRK also includes ten dynamic sequences spanning multiple surgical tasks, with increasing kinematic complexity and tissue deformation, providing a basis for future research in multi-viewpoint surgical perception. The project is available at: https://mv-dvrk.is.mpg.de.

</details>


### [42] [DPA: Decoupling Product-Agnostic Anomaly Representations for Zero-shot Anomaly Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02075)
*Hang Yao,Yansheng Fu,Ming Liu,Zifei Yan,Yanli Ji,Hongzhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于异常迁移的零样本异常生成方法，利用已有产品的真实异常来为新产品生成逼真的异常样本，无需新产品的异常数据。新方法提升了异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测依赖异常样本，但新产品上线时往往只有正常图片，异常样本难以获取。现有的零样本异常生成通常依赖纹理图片或文本描述，但生成的异常常不真实。因此，寻找更贴合实际的异常生成方式非常重要。

Method: 提出异常迁移的零样本生成，通过筛选与目标产品相符的异常类型，从已有产品转移真实异常。核心为DPA扩散框架，利用不匹配数据对训练，获得产品无关的异常嵌入，实现跨产品异常概念迁移。设计自适应掩码机制控制异常生成的合理位置和形态，并引入免训练的异常标注模块进行像素级异常标注。

Result: 在MVTec-AD、VisA及自建异常迁移基准上的大量实验表明，所提方法生成的异常更真实，并在零样本和小样本异常检测任务中实现明显性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文方法突破了常规异常生成局限，通过跨产品异常迁移，兼顾异常生成的真实性与可用性，为工业异常检测的实际应用带来显著价值。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection benefits from anomaly samples, yet newly deployed products typically provide only normal images, making anomaly samples difficult to collect. Zero-shot anomaly generation offers a promising solution which avoids collection of target-product anomalies. However, existing methods mainly rely on texture images or text descriptions as anomaly sources, which often produce unrealistic anomalies. Observing that similar anomalies can recur across different products, we propose anomaly transfer-based zero-shot generation, which reuses real anomalies from existing source products, making target-product anomalies no longer necessary to generate realistic anomalious samples for unseen target products. Since not every anomaly type suits the target product, an anomaly type filtering mechanism first selects plausible source types. To transfer selected anomaly, we propose DPA, a diffusion-based framework that decouples product-agnostic anomaly representations. Instead of directly extracting anomaly representations, DPA learns product-irrelevant anomaly embeddings through training with the mismatched data pair, enabling transferable anomaly concept learning across products. Furthermore, we design an adaptive mask-guided pipeline that leverages adaptive masks to control the positional and geometric plausibility of generated anomalies during generation. A training-free anomaly labeling module is further introduced to produce pixel-level annotations aligned with generated anomalies. Extensive experiments on MVTec-AD, VisA, and a dedicated anomaly-transfer benchmark demonstrate that the proposed setting and DPA generate more realistic anomalies and significantly improve downstream anomaly detection performance under both zero-shot and few-shot settings. Source code and models will be released.

</details>

*Zhouyuan Huai,Meiqi Wan,Yan Yang,Minshi Chen,Xin Yuan,Wei Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型的3D船舶检测方法KSG-Net，显著提升了在复杂海事环境下对不同尺度船舶的检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前海事环境下的自动导航对高精度3D船舶检测有很高需求，但海上目标变化大、小型目标点云稀疏以及强干扰下，现有2D或稠密建模方法难以兼顾精度与效率，且现有适用于陆地的稀疏3D方法泛化性差。

Method: 提出KSG-Net网络，包括两个核心模块：1）KSMA模块通过选取关键信息体素并进行多尺度特征聚合，提升对小型、稀疏船只的检测能力；2）GCA模块通过全局上下文建模，强化对大型船舶几何结构的理解。整体框架为端到端稀疏检测器，兼顾局部判别性和全局结构感知。

Result: 在Thames River船舶数据集及仿真数据上，KSG-Net无论在多尺度检测精度还是在复杂环境中的鲁棒性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: KSG-Net有效解决了海事3D检测中小目标弱特征与大目标全局建模不足的问题，为实际自动导航提供了更强的技术支持。

Abstract: Accurate 3D ship detection in maritime environments is critical for autonomous navigation, yet remains challenging due to large-scale vessel variations, sparse point clouds of small vessels, and severe sea-clutter interference. Existing methods, primarily based on 2D features or dense representations, struggle to balance detection accuracy and computational efficiency, while sparse 3D detectors designed for road scenes generalize poorly to maritime scenarios. This paper focuses on two key challenges in maritime LiDAR perception: weak feature representation for small and sparse vessels, and insufficient global structural modeling for large vessels due to the limited receptive field of local sparse convolutions. To address these issues, we propose KSG-Net, a Key-Sparse and Global-Context learning network for maritime 3D ship detection. The core idea is to jointly enhance local discriminative features and global structural awareness within a unified fully sparse detection framework. Specifically, a Key Sparse Multi-scale Aggregation (KSMA) module is designed to enhance the representation of small and sparse vessels by selecting informative key voxels and aggregating cross-scale neighborhood features. Furthermore, a Global Context Aggregation (GCA) module is introduced to capture long-range geometric dependencies through scene-level context modeling with gated residual interactions, thereby improving the representation of large vessels. Extensive experiments on the Thames River vessel dataset and simulated datasets demonstrate that KSG-Net consistently outperforms existing methods in multi-scale vessel detection and exhibits strong robustness in complex maritime environments.

</details>

*Yilong Guo,Hanqi Chen,Zixiao Ye,Guanzhong Wang,Chen Yu,Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可执行式前端网页生成代理RILA，能通过多模态输入生成功能正确且美观的交互式网页，显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型生成网页主要侧重视觉层面，缺乏对网页交互功能正确性的有效检验。为了提升生成网页的可用性和真实可交互性，亟需自动化且可靠的交互性验证和优化方法。

Method: 提出RILA（可执行驱动代理），将浏览器实际渲染与执行环节纳入生成流程。核心包括AIV（动作交互验证）模块，通过重放参考交互轨迹收集执行反馈，以及ERS（执行感知渲染得分）综合评价交互正确率与视觉还原度，指导生成代码的循环优化。此外还构建自动执行验证的数据合成流水线用于高质量训练。

Result: 在IWR-Bench基准上，RILA系统性提升了所有基础模型的交互正确性和视觉还原度，训练管线使小规模Qwen3.5-9B模型的指标从40.40%提升至57.52%，超越了更大参数量的主流模型。

Conclusion: RILA通过引入可执行反馈与交互验证，弥补了多模态大模型网页生成中交互性弱的短板，并提供数据、评测和方法的全链路技术方案，为实用级网页生成奠定基础。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have achieved remarkable progress in front-end web development, generating interactive webpages from multimodal references such as screenshots and interaction videos. However, existing work largely emphasizes visual metrics such as aesthetics and layout similarity, while overlooking the more critical validation of interactive functionality. We present RILA, an execution-driven agent that puts browser rendering in the loop, iteratively editing generated code from runtime interaction feedback. RILA introduces an Action Interaction Verification (AIV) module that replays the reference interaction trajectory on the generated webpage to collect grounded execution-aware observations, and an Execution-aware Rendering Score (ERS) that jointly measures interaction correctness and visual fidelity to guide iterative optimization. We further build an execution-verified data synthesis pipeline that produces diverse, high-quality training data, offering gains complementary to inference-time optimization. On IWR-Bench, RILA consistently improves both interaction and visual fidelity across foundation models. Notably, with our training pipeline, RILA lifts the compact Qwen3.5-9B backbone from 40.40% to 57.52%, surpassing far larger one-shot generators, including the 1T-parameter Kimi-K2.6 (55.61%) and the proprietary GPT-5.5 (55.74%).

</details>


### [45] [Evidence-Guided Detection, Localization and Explanation for Text-Centric Image Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02097)
*Peifeng Liu,Bin Li,Qingsong Zhang,Yangxin Yu,Leqing Chen,Xiaoye Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一套用于检测和解释AIGC生成图像篡改的证据引导系统，并在GenText-Forensics Challenge取得了第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIGC发展使基于文本的图像编辑更为普及，随之带来新的取证难题：不仅需要判断真伪，还要定位篡改区域并生成有据可依的解释。

Method: 系统包含三大模块：全局判真伪的检测器、可定位篡改区域的定位器，以及基于多模态大模型（MLLM）的推理器，它们依次串联。引入难度感知矿池提升定位质量，并通过报告-掩码一致性后处理优化最终输出。

Result: 本系统在官方隐藏测试集中获得0.638分，排名挑战赛第二，表现优异。

Conclusion: 所提证据引导系统有效提升了图像篡改取证能力，为AIGC内容取证提供了新思路。

Abstract: The rapid progress of AIGC has made text-centric image manipulation increasingly accessible, creating new forensic challenges that require not only authenticity detection but also spatial grounding and evidence-based explanation. This paper presents our solution to the GenText-Forensics Challenge at ACM Multimedia 2026. We propose an evidence-guided detector-localizer-reasoner system, where an image-level detector provides a global authenticity prior, a dedicated localizer extracts tampered regions as spatial grounding evidence, and an MLLM-based reasoner generates structured forensic reports grounded in this expert forensic evidence. These modules are connected through a cascaded evidence flow: the detector gates the subsequent localization and prompting process, the localizer converts tamper responses into grounding boxes, and the reasoner is trained to synthesize the detector decision and localized evidence into the final report. As a key part of our method, we introduce iterative difficulty-aware mining to improve localization quality and apply report-mask consistency post-processing to align report grounding with predicted masks. On the official hidden test set, our system achieves a final score of 0.638 and ranks second in the challenge, validating the effectiveness of the proposed evidence-guided system. The code is available at https://github.com/peifengLiu42/ACMMM26-evidence-guided-detector-localizer-reasoner-system.

</details>


### [46] [Disease Burden over Skin Tone: Decomposing the Dermatology-AI Generalization Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02111)
*Nirajan Kunwor,Sanjaya Poudel,Quoc-Huy Trinh,Jahidul Arafat,Sunil Kumar Gaire*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了皮肤病AI模型泛化性差的原因，发现疾病分布的变化比肤色多样性对模型表现影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有皮肤病AI模型主要在白人皮肤和癌症图像上训练，部署时常遇到与训练集肤色和疾病类型均不同的患者，急需理解哪些差异导致模型泛化性下降。

Method: 作者评估了癌症训练的基线模型（ResNet-50微调于HAM10000和ISIC 2019）、两种皮肤科领域基础模型（DermLIP和MONET）和通用视觉模型（DINOv3），分别作为特征提取器。在肤色分层且疾病类型匹配的数据集（Diverse Dermatology Images, DDI）和疾病类型不同但肤色多样的数据集（Skin Condition Image Network, SCIN）上测试和分析。

Result: 实验显示，疾病分布迁移使基线准确率从0.62降至0.21，而同一疾病不同肤色间差距较小（0.10-0.18）且不一致。特征分析显示，癌症模型在新疾病下表现差、特征结构性弱，皮肤病基础模型有更强迁移结构。同时，领域基础模型借助每类疾病约10个有标签样本能快速恢复大部分性能。

Conclusion: 疾病分布迁移比肤色多样性更严重影响皮肤病AI泛化。合理利用领域基础模型，并进行轻量微调，有助于提升泛化能力。还公开了评估协议和代码，推动皮肤病AI模型的公平性与可复现分析。

Abstract: Dermatology artificial intelligence (AI) models are predominantly trained on light-skinned, cancer-focused image collections, yet they are increasingly proposed for deployment in resource-constrained settings where patients differ from training populations along two confounded axes: skin tone and disease distribution. We investigate whether poor generalization is primarily caused by skin-tone underrepresentation or disease-distribution shift. We evaluate a cancer-trained baseline (ResNet-50 fine-tuned on HAM10000 and ISIC 2019), two dermatology foundation models (DermLIP and MONET), and a general-purpose vision model (DINOv3) as frozen feature extractors. Models are evaluated on a tone-stratified disease-matched dataset (Diverse Dermatology Images, DDI) and a disease-shifted tone-diverse dataset (Skin Condition Image Network, SCIN). Our results show that disease-distribution shift contributes more than skin tone in the evaluated settings. The cancer baseline decreases from 0.62 to 0.21 balanced accuracy when transferred to unfamiliar clinical conditions, while the within-disease skin-tone gap is smaller (0.10-0.18) and inconsistent. Label-free representation analysis shows that this failure reflects a representational limitation rather than only missing output labels: cancer-specialized features poorly cluster unfamiliar conditions (kNN purity lift +0.06 over chance), whereas dermatology-pretrained features retain stronger transferable structure (+0.23). Finally, we show that representation quality predicts recoverable performance under lightweight adaptation. Starting from dermatology foundation models, approximately ten labeled examples per clinical category recover most attainable performance. We release the evaluation protocol and code to support reproducible auditing of dermatology AI generalization.

</details>


### [47] [Synergistic Information Disentanglement for Omni-modal Slide Representation Learning in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02118)
*Mingxin Liu,Chengfei Cai,Anwen Lu,Pengbo Xu,Jun Li,Jinze Li,Depin Chen,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了一种新的全模态自监督学习方法（Φ-Omni），通过拆解和增强不同病理数据模态（如组织学、基因组学和临床报告）之间的协同信息，实现更具泛化能力的病理切片表征。该方法能有效保留交叉模态独特诊断信号，提升多任务少样本学习表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态表示学习方法常通过对比学习让不同数据模态在同一潜在空间对齐，但这会造成模态坍缩，即模态间独特和协同（synergistic）诊断信号被忽略，影响下游任务表现。作者认为应将多模态间的协同信息作为表征学习的核心来源，而不是简单对齐冗余部分。

Method: 提出Φ-Omni框架，基于Partial Information Decomposition（PID）理论，通过Synergistic Information Bottleneck（SIB）和自定义的ΦID损失目标，有效抑制冗余信息，最大化不可约协同信息，提炼不同模态间的高阶交互。

Result: 在乳腺和肺癌数据集上预训练的Φ-Omni，在5个外部数据集、8项任务上，相较传统监督和自监督方法显示出更强的少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 明确证明了挖掘和利用多模态数据中的协同信号对于提升图像表征和少样本迁移表现的重要性，Φ-Omni为病理图像分析的多模态表征学习提供了新的方向。

Abstract: In computational pathology (CPath), developing omni-modal self-supervised learning (SSL) models that integrate histology, genomics, and clinical reports enables transferable representation learning for whole slide images (WSIs). Existing approaches implicitly force heterogeneous modalities into a uniform latent space by contrastive alignment, causing modality collapse where unique, synergistic diagnostic signals (termed as $\mathrmΦ$) are discarded in favor of trivial redundancy. We hypothesize that the strongest task-agnostic SSL training signal stems from distilling the synergistic interactions over merely aligning shared redundancy. To this end, we introduce \textsc{$\mathrmΦ$-Omni}, a synergistic information disentanglement framework grounded in Partial Information Decomposition (PID) theory for slide representation learning. Unlike standard contrastive approaches, \textsc{$\mathrmΦ$-Omni} employs a Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SIB) regulated by the proposed $\mathrmΦ\text{ID}$ objective, which explicitly suppresses marginal redundancy while maximizing irreducible synergy, thereby distilling high-order cross-modal interactions. Following pretraining on breast ($n$=1031) and lung ($n$=919) cohorts, \textsc{$\mathrmΦ$-Omni} demonstrates superior few-shot performance across five independent external datasets spanning eight tasks compared to supervised and SSL baselines. Source code is available here.

</details>


### [48] [World-Coherent Decoding: Self-Verifying Test-Time Planning for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02159)
*Chuhan Zhang,Seiji Ito,Kenta Hoshino,Satoshi Ikehata,Ikuro Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的方法WCD（World-Coherent-Decoding），通过在机器人视觉行动预测领域为每次动作规划挑选更可靠的想象未来，实现更稳定、有效的机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Models（WAMs）通过生成多个未来进行动作推理，但结果易受选中的未来影响，缺乏鲁棒性和可靠性。为提升行动可靠性，需要一种机制来自动挑选更可信赖的未来。

Method: WCD方法在每次决策时，从已训练的WAM模型中采样多个未来--行动候选项，并通过视觉合理性（视频惊奇度flow-based surprisal）和行为稳定性（行动路径effort）对其进行内部打分排序。实际执行后，再用结果与想象进行对比修正，并用轻量预测器辅助优化未来选择，但不对主模型进行更新。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0平台上，WCD方法在有限监督场景下将Hard成功率从55.80%提高到60.90%，在Horizon-3任务上改进16.43个百分点，并在真实机器人视觉偏移测试中表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 实验表明，仅靠多生成预测并不能提升WAM性能，关键在于挑选可靠的未来规划。WCD有效前置自验证机制，提高了机器人执行的可靠性和稳定性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) aim to control robots by stochastically generating visual futures and then decoding actions, but empirical observations indicate that the results can strongly depend on which future is selected. We propose World-Coherent-Decoding (WCD), a self-verifying test-time planning framework that treats WAM rollouts as falsifiable future--action hypotheses. At each decision step, WCD samples multiple candidates from a frozen WAM and ranks them using internal generative signals: flow-based video surprisal for visual plausibility and action path effort for action-generation stability. After execution, the realized observation audits the selected imagination, yielding an imagination--reality mismatch that trains a lightweight online predictor for future candidate selection. Thus, WCD converts delayed self-verification into pre-execution reliability estimation without updating the backbone model. On RoboTwin 2.0, WCD improves Hard success under limited randomized-scene supervision from $55.80\%$ to $60.90\%$, with a $+16.43$ gains on Horizon-3 tasks, and shows qualitative robustness on real Franka visual-shift tests. These results highlight a simple principle: test-time scaling for WAMs depends less on sampling more futures than on selecting reliable ones.

</details>


### [49] [Progressive Pseudo-Label Optimization for Point-Supervised Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02171)
*Hailong Ning,Hao Wang,Yimeng Wang,Tao Lei,Renwei Dian,Asoke K. Nandi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合SAM2先验和自训练框架的点监督变化检测(PS-CD)新方法，实现了仅用稀疏点标注下对影像变化的高质量像素级检测，性能优于已有弱监督方法，并与部分全监督方法竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点监督变化检测极大减少了标注成本，但稀疏点标注易导致伪标签不完整和噪声多，限制了方法精度。因此，需要一种新框架提升伪标签质量和检测精度。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段用SAM2利用点标注生成面向目标的候选掩码，通过双时相掩码选择和CNN精细化模块（带不确定性感知损失）提升伪标签质量；第二阶段构建教师-学生自训练，教师模型用滑动平均更新，不断刷新伪标签，实现伪标签精化和模型再优化的闭环优化。

Result: 在WHU-CD、LEVIR-CD和SYSU-CD三个基准数据集上实验，所提方法绝大部分指标优于现有弱监督方法，并与多种全监督方法相当。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效提升点监督变化检测的精度和伪标签质量，兼具弱标注低成本和高性能优势，为变化检测任务提供了高性价比的新思路。

Abstract: Point-supervised change detection (PS-CD) aims to identify pixel-level changes between bi-temporal images using only sparsely annotated points. Although point annotations substantially reduce labeling costs, their limited spatial coverage often results in incomplete and noisy pseudo-labels. To address this issue, we propose a two-stage framework that introduces SAM2 priors into PS-CD and progressively adapts them to the target task. In Stage I, SAM2 generates object-aware candidate masks from point annotations on the bi-temporal images, and a bi-temporal mask selection strategy is designed to convert generic segmentation responses into more reliable change pseudo-labels. Subsequently, a lightweight CNN refinement module with an uncertainty-aware loss is employed to improve boundary quality and local structural consistency. In Stage II, we construct a teacher-student self-training framework in which the teacher is updated by exponential moving average and periodically refreshes the pseudo-labels. This design establishes a closed-loop optimization process that alternates between pseudo-label refinement and model re-optimization. Experiments on three benchmark datasets, including WHU-CD, LEVIR-CD, and SYSU-CD, demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms previous weakly supervised approaches on most benchmarks and remains competitive with several fully supervised methods.

</details>


### [50] [CC-4DGS: Computational Deformation and Point-Cloud Compression for Storage-Efficient Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02184)
*Kyungdae Park,Chae Eun Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的4D高斯投影表达方法CC-4DGS，能大幅减少单场景存储（降至20-30MB），同时保持高质量和实时渲染性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态4D高斯投影方法存储占用大，难以扩展。急需能够压缩存储的高效表达以便于更广泛应用。

Method: 1）提出计算变形场（CDF），用致密哈希编码和紧凑神经解码器替代大规模可学习哈希表，实现变形特征的即时高效合成；2）提出点云属性压缩（CCA）流水线，通过条件自编码、选择性量化和残差码本实现点云特征高效压缩。

Result: 在N3DV和Technicolor Light Field数据集上，经大量实验，CC-4DGS与当前最强方法（如Swift4D）相比，在重建精度相当的情况下，显著提升了存储效率并优化了内存-运行时的权衡。

Conclusion: CC-4DGS能极大降低场景存储需求，同时保持实时性能和高画质，有望促进高质量4D视图合成方法的规模化应用。

Abstract: Dynamic four-dimensional (4D) Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a powerful explicit representation for high-quality view synthesis, yet existing methods still require tens to hundreds of megabytes per scene due to their heavy reliance on large multi-resolution hash tables and high-dimensional Gaussian attributes. This paper presents CC-4DGS, a storage-efficient and scalable framework that rethinks both deformation modeling and canonical attribute storage. First, we introduce a computational deformation field (CDF) that replaces large multi-resolution learnable hash tables with deterministic dense hash encoding and compact neural decoders, enabling on-the-fly synthesis of deformation features while reducing deformation storage to only 1--3 MB per scene. Second, we propose a compression of canonical point-cloud attributes (CCA) pipeline that compresses high-dimensional spherical harmonic appearance terms and auxiliary Gaussian attributes via conditional autoencoding, selective quantization, and residual codebooks, achieving 3--5$\times$ point-cloud reduction with negligible quality loss. Together, these components yield a unified representation that preserves real-time rendering performance while reducing total storage to 20--30 MB. Extensive experiments across the N3DV and Technicolor Light Field datasets demonstrate that CC-4DGS achieves reconstruction accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art methods such as Swift4D, while offering significantly improved storage efficiency and favorable runtime-memory trade-offs.

</details>


### [51] [Lightweight Adaptation of General-Purpose VLMs for Multispectral and SAR Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02187)
*Shanji Liu,Kelu Yao,Junxiao Xue,Chenghui Lv,Xiangyang Miao,Yekai Huang,Yaying Chen,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级方法，将通用视觉-语言模型（VLM）适配多光谱和合成孔径雷达（SAR）数据，无需训练新的基础模型，通过渲染多视图输入和LoRA技术实现高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前多数预训练视觉编码器仅支持三通道自然图像，难以直接处理多光谱或SAR观测数据。为将强大的VLM能力迁移至这些特殊遥感任务，降低定制编码器和额外预训练的需求，亟需简单高效的适配方法。

Method: 作者利用VLM多图像接口，将每次观测渲染为五个不同的光学视图和一个SAR视图，在文本提示中明确标注，并用LoRA适配语言网络及部分视觉Transformer模块。通过结构化监督和偏好对，结合观测证据提升模型的学习和泛化能力。

Result: 在BigEarthNet-v2六分类地表覆盖基准上，改造后的Qwen3-VL取得了0.8275的micro F1分数。同样协议应用于其他四种VLM架构也有提升，在Sen1Floods11洪水验证与BigEarthNet.txt描述任务上也迁移有效。消融试验证明模型确实利用了提供的多通道观测。

Conclusion: VLM可通过渲染输入和LoRA适配被有效迁移用于多光谱和SAR任务，无需从头训练新模型，为通用视觉-语言模型在遥感领域的应用提供了高效快速的适配路径。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language models (VLMs) now support strong visual recognition, instruction following, and generation. However, most pretrained visual encoders are built around three-channel natural images and do not directly accommodate observations such as native multispectral measurements or synthetic aperture radar (SAR). Adapting VLMs to these sensors typically requires dedicated encoders and domain pretraining, slowing the reuse of stronger general-purpose checkpoints. We show that the multi-image interface of general-purpose VLMs offers a lightweight alternative. Our protocol renders each observation as five optical views and one SAR view, names them in the prompt, and adapts the language network and selected visual transformer blocks with LoRA. This exposes band composites, spectral indices, and radar backscatter through an existing visual interface. For land-cover recognition, structured supervision couples predicted classes with sensor evidence. We further construct preference pairs in which a true label is omitted while its supporting evidence is retained, encouraging complete predictions that remain consistent with the observations. On a balanced six-class land-cover benchmark derived from BigEarthNet-v2, the adapted Qwen3-VL reaches 0.8275 micro F1. The same input and adaptation protocol improves all four tested VLM architectures and transfers to Sen1Floods11 flood verification and BigEarthNet.txt captioning. Image removal and mismatch controls show that the adapted models use the supplied sensor observations. Together, these results demonstrate that VLMs can be repurposed for multispectral and SAR tasks through rendered inputs and compact LoRA adaptation, without training a new foundation model.

</details>


### [52] [TAME: Temporal-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Text-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02204)
*Uicheol Jung,Juyoung Hong,Hojung Kwon,Yukyung Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了TAME框架，有效提升了基于CLIP的视频检索性能，主要通过引入MoE、FT tokens与CTIA模块，实现更好的时序建模和句-视频对齐，多个数据集均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前图文对齐的模型（如CLIP）在用于视频检索(Text-Video Retrieval, TVR)时缺乏时序建模能力，只考虑整体特征会丢失时间信息，难以应对视频帧之间的异质性和动态变化。

Method: 1) 在CLIP编码器中加入稀疏Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)层，并对视觉端采取帧一致路由，让专家对帧级模式分工。2) 引入Frame-Temporal (FT) token，聚合帧间全局信息，并回馈到每帧，捕捉长距离时序依赖。3) 设计Cross-Temporal Interaction and Aggregation (CTIA)模块，对句-视频帧相似度通过阶段性时序过滤与融合进行细致优化。

Result: 在MSR-VTT等标准视频检索基准上，TAME相比基于CLIP的baseline有显著提升。例如在MSR-VTT数据集上，R@1比CLIP4Clip提升4.0个百分点，在DiDeMo、MSVD、LSMDC和ActivityNet等数据集也取得了稳健进步。

Conclusion: TAME框架通过结合多专家分工、全局/局部时序建模与多粒度相似度优化，提升文本—视频检索效果，为视频多模态表征和检索提供更优方法。

Abstract: Text-Video Retrieval (TVR) retrieves videos that match a natural-language query, but extending image-text models such as CLIP to videos is fundamentally limited by the lack of temporal modeling. Videos exhibit frame-wise heterogeneity in appearance and motion, and compressing all frames into a single representation often obscures temporal structure and semantic transitions. To address this, we propose Temporal-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Text-Video Retrieval (TAME), a CLIP-based framework that jointly models frame-level structure and temporal relations. First, we integrate sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers into both CLIP encoders and apply frame-consistent routing on the vision branch so that experts specialize according to frame-level visual patterns while preserving the original vision-language alignment. Second, we introduce Frame-Temporal (FT) tokens that aggregate global cross-frame information and feed it back to each frame, enabling the visual encoder to capture long-range temporal dependencies without harming local details. Third, we design a Cross-Temporal Interaction and Aggregation (CTIA) module that refines frame-wise sentence-video similarities through staged temporal filtering and fusion. Experiments on standard TVR benchmarks show that TAME consistently improves over CLIP-based baselines. On MSR-VTT, it improves R@1 by 4.0 over CLIP4Clip, and also achieves consistent gains on DiDeMo, MSVD, LSMDC, and ActivityNet. The code is available at https://github.com/sejong-rcv/TAME.

</details>


### [53] [LeakageBench: Document-Level Leakage Risk for Redacting Personally Identifiable Information in Document Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02207)
*Vishnu Prasad Vijaya Kumar,Santhosh Venkatesh,Ivan P. Yamshchikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出LeakageBench数据集，专注于图片化文档的PII去识别任务，评估现有方法并揭示即使F1提升，页面整体安全性问题仍未有效解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PII去识别多以文本为主，忽视了文档图片（如扫描件、截图、PDF等）中的实际脱敏难题，尤其是OCR误差、页面结构和视觉噪声对敏感信息遗漏的影响。缺乏专门面向图片文档的高风险红线评测基准。

Method: 提出LeakageBench——包含500个文档图片、11,954个GDPR对齐的PII标注，分为直接标识符、链接密钥和上下文重识别三类。评估通用OCR管线、商用/定制化OCR依赖检测器及OCR-free视觉-语言模型，采用实体级F1、组级泄漏和文档级泄漏三类指标。

Result: 如Code Interpreter模型将GPT-5.5的定位F1从0.090提升到0.249，但页级泄漏率仍高达0.968，说明即便在检测准确率提高时，大多数页面仍不安全。

Conclusion: 增强检测与工具仅改善了定位，但多数文档页面距离安全发布仍有显著差距。LeakageBench为高召回、空间定位精准的PII去识别模型提供了新的权威诊断基准。

Abstract: Real-world personally identifiable information (PII) redaction often operates on document images---scans, screenshots, and PDF renderings---where OCR errors, layout structure, and visual noise determine whether sensitive information is actually removed. Existing PII benchmarks are mostly text-centric and do not measure document-level redaction risk: a page remains unsafe if even one identifier is missed. We introduce LeakageBench, a challenge set of 500 document images with 11,954 GDPR-aligned PII annotations spanning direct identifiers, linkage keys, and contextual re-identification surfaces. We evaluate generic OCR pipelines, commercial and task-adapted OCR-dependent detectors, and OCR-free vision-language models using entity-level F1, group-wise leakage, and document-level leakage metrics. Code Interpreter raises GPT-5.5 localization F1 from 0.090 to 0.249, but critical page-level leakage remains 0.968. These results show that stronger detection and tool assistance improve localization without making most pages safe for release. LeakageBench provides a diagnostic benchmark for high-recall, spatially grounded PII redaction in document images.

</details>


### [54] [Asymmetric Paired-Annotation Learning for Multi-Structure ULF Pediatric Brain MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02210)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Dang P. M. Cao,Minh Hoang Pham,Khanh Nguyen Vo Ngoc,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于nnU-Net的不对称监督策略AURA，旨在提升低场（0.064T）MRI儿童神经影像分割的表现，尤其在解剖结构界限弱和配准误差大的情况下。通过结合高场和低场标注信息，AURA在挑战赛开发集上取得与高场基线接近的分割准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
