Among the few consistent findings is that the selection of persona attributes matters, and that using more attributes does not necessarily lead to better performance. It remains unclear how different attribute selection methods perform and how to choose among them. In this paper, we propose that observed human response variation of a survey question is a potential explanation for the mixed performance observed so far. In addition, we compare the performance of persona prompting associated with different methods for selecting persona attributes. We evaluate these methods on four different (general) social surveys across two countries, six LLMs, and twenty prediction tasks per survey. Our work helps to identify when persona prompting can be expected to be useful in survey prediction tasks, and provides new insights on the effectiveness of different attribute selection methods for LLM-based survey prediction using persona prompting.

</details>


### [164] [Predictors of Loneliness in Older Adults Using Multimodal Analysis of Speech and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02606)
*Vinmay Khandode,Sai Karthik Kosuri,Neil K. R. Sehgal,Adam Greene,Elif Alpoge,Elana Duffy,Matthew Lee Smith,Thomas K. M. Cudjoe,Sharath Chandra Guntuku*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过电话面试分析了310位老年人的语言和语音特征，发现孤独感可以通过语言和语音线索在对话中体现。多模态模型（结合语言和语音特征）在检测孤独感方面优于单一模型，有望辅助心理健康评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人孤独感普遍且危害健康，现有检测手段缺乏客观性及可大规模应用的能力，尤其是在自然对话场景中。研究旨在探索通过普通对话检测孤独感的方法，为心理健康干预提供新工具。

Method: 招募310位老年人进行半结构化电话访谈，采集文本和语音数据。利用心理语言学词典、N-gram、主题模型等提取语言特征，结合音高、语调、音量等声学特征，构建多模态分析框架，关联自报孤独感评分，统计分析不同孤独感水平下的语言变化。

Result: 高度孤独感与否定、负面语调及冲突相关词汇正相关；低孤独感与社会关系、动机、情感丰富度负相关。多模态模型检测孤独感（r=0.298）优于仅文本或仅语音。

Conclusion: 孤独感可通过语言与声学特征在日常对话中有效捕捉。语音分析能为孤独检测和心理调研提供客观补充，但目前更适合作为辅助工具而非独立诊断方案。

Abstract: Loneliness is a critical public health issue among older adults, linked to higher risks of depression, cognitive decline, and mortality. Scalable, objective methods for its detection remain limited, particularly in natural conversational contexts. We analyzed speech and language markers of loneliness in 310 older adults using semi-structured telephone interviews to help understand how they process feeling lonely and how their language differs at different levels of feeling loneliness. Our multimodal framework combined linguistic features (psycholinguistic dictionaries, n-grams, and topic models) with acoustic features (pitch, tone, loudness) to examine associations with self-reported loneliness scores. Both predefined and data-driven methods captured patterns in verbal content and vocal delivery. Higher loneliness was associated with negations(r = 0.11), negative tone(r = 0.12), and conflict-related language. Lower loneliness was linked to social references(r = -0.18), motivational drives(r = -0.11), and emotional richness in speech(r = -0.12). We also found that the multimodal model (r = 0.298) outperforms the text-only and audio-only models. Findings suggest that loneliness manifests through both linguistic and acoustic cues, supporting the potential of speech-based analysis in psychological assessments and as an early indicator of emotional loneliness when used alongside existing assessments, rather than as standalone diagnostic tools.

</details>


### [165] [TaRA: Training-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02639)
*Taehyeon Kim,Eunhyeok Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种新的LoRA初始化方法——TaRA，能够提升PEFT中的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LoRA初始化方法未能充分考虑全秩模型训练过程中的梯度变化，导致低秩分解下的信息瓶颈影响性能，因此需要更贴合训练动力学的初始化方法。

Method: 提出TaRA方法，在初始化LoRA时，使低秩因子产生的梯度尽量接近全秩权重矩阵的梯度。该方法通过数学推导实现，并保持计算开销极低。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的微调任务中，TaRA在表现上全面优于现有的LoRA初始化方法。

Conclusion: TaRA为LoRA初始化提供了一种简单、稳健且可扩展的新方案，有效提升了PEFT的表现。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a de facto standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), yet its performance is highly sensitive to initialization due to the information bottleneck imposed by low-rank decomposition. Existing approaches attempt to construct high-quality LoRA initializations by exploiting principal components of pretrained weights, activations, or gradients. However, these methods do not directly account for the training dynamics of the full-rank model. In this paper, we propose Training-aware Low-Rank Adaptation Initialization (TaRA), a method that initializes LoRA such that the gradients induced by the low-rank factors closely approximate the gradient of the corresponding full-rank weight matrix. Derived from a mathematical formulation, TaRA improves gradient fidelity at the start of training while introducing negligible computational overhead. Across diverse and challenging fine-tuning tasks, TaRA consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods, establishing a simple, robust, and scalable solution for effective LoRA initialization.

</details>


### [166] [WinoQueer-NL: Assessing Bias in Dutch Language Models toward LGBTQ+ Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02651)
*Jiska Beuk,Gerasimos Spanakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文针对荷兰语模型中的反同性恋偏见进行了系统研究，提出并验证了一个荷兰语专用的数据集，并用其评测了多种模型中的群体偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前，对英文语言模型中的反同性恋偏见已有较多研究，但荷兰语模型相关工作不多。本文旨在填补荷兰语领域反同性恋和跨性别偏见评估的空白。

Method: 作者基于英文WinoQueer基准，开发出适应荷兰文化和语言的数据集，包括刻板与反刻板配对句。通过43名荷兰酷儿参与的问卷，验证了大部分刻板印象的文化相关性，并发现新偏见类型。最终数据集含4万余句，并用多种荷兰及多语种语言模型（MLM、ARLM）进行测试，分析模型在刻板与反刻板句的偏好（通过对数似然比打分）。

Result: 整体看，模型表现偏好接近中性（约50%），但细致分析发现存在明显分化：某些模型在处理跨性别群体时高达97%的偏向刻板句，而在针对男同性恋配对时仅有6%；跨性别和非二元身份相关内容普遍被赋予最高偏见分。

Conclusion: 本文强调基于文化土壤构建数据集的重要性，该方法有助于发现和缓解荷兰语模型中对边缘群体的偏见，并为评测多语种模型提供参考。

Abstract: While English language models have been widely examined for anti-queer bias, Dutch models remain understudied. To address this gap, we developed a culturally and linguistically adapted Dutch dataset based on the English WinoQueer benchmark, containing pairs of stereotypical and counter-stereotypical sentences. To validate and expand it, we conducted an online survey with 43 Dutch queer participants, confirming 145 of 171 stereotypes as culturally relevant and identifying 22 new biases through free-text responses. The final released dataset, comprising 42,906 sentences, was evaluated using a range of Dutch-specific and multilingual models, including both masked language models (MLMs) and autoregressive language models (ARLMs), with bias measured via a score comparing log-likelihoods of stereotypical versus counter-stereotypical sentences. While the mean bias score across models appeared neutral (~50%), closer analysis revealed significant disparities: some models favored stereotypical sentences up to 97% of the time for transgender identities, but only 6% of the time for gay-related pairs, with transgender and non-binary identities consistently receiving the highest bias scores. Our findings highlight the importance of culturally grounded datasets for evaluating and mitigating biases that disproportionately impact marginalized groups in Dutch language models.

</details>


### [167] [oHC: Orthogonal Hyper-Connections on SO(4) via Quaternions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02672)
*Haoqiang Guo,Xuyi Chen,Bo Ke,Yishu Lei,Ziyang Xu,Shikun Feng,Ximen,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的Transformer结构——超连结（Hyper-Connections，HC），将单一残差流拓展为多个并行流，并通过正交约束实现更稳定与高效的训练表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer仅用单一残差流，且现有多流混合方式存在训练不稳定、梯度放大或衰减等问题，影响模型性能和深度可扩展性。

Method: 作者提出HC结构，将残差流拓展为n个并行通路，通过n×n残差混合矩阵融合信息。为解决混合矩阵无约束带来的梯度不稳定，设计了两种约束：mHC（约束为双随机矩阵，限制放大但存衰减）；oHC（约束为正交矩阵，具体为SO(n)旋转群），既不放大也不衰减，确保训练稳定且残差流多样性得以保留。并用四流情况下闭式参数化旋转，不增额外参数且计算高效。

Result: oHC在多个下游任务上进行了全面评测，优于单流残差、mHC及iHC（恒等残差）等基线方法，表现出更好的稳定性和性能。

Conclusion: 正交约束的Hyper-Connections有效提高了多流残差Transformer的训练稳定性和模型性能，为深层Transformer设计提供了更优选择。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) replace the single residual stream of a Transformer with $n$ parallel ones, mixing them at every layer with a learned $n \times n$ residual matrix. Leaving that matrix unconstrained places no limit on the factor by which the mixing step rescales the residual streams, and that factor compounds across layers, which destabilizes training. Manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) address this by restricting the matrix to the doubly stochastic matrices. That caps the factor at one, so the mixing can no longer amplify any direction, but nothing bounds it from below. We prove that inside this set the mixing step can reduce the norm of the residual streams only by shrinking the differences between the streams, while their mean is left unchanged; and since the reduction accumulates over layers, the streams grow more alike and their diversity is spent with depth. We therefore propose Orthogonal Hyper-Connections (oHC), restricting the residual matrix to the rotation group $SO(n)$, so that the mixing step can neither amplify nor attenuate the residual streams in any direction, which keeps training stable and no longer forces the differences between the streams to contract. Specifically, at the four streams used by recent HC models we parameterize the group in closed form by a pair of unit quaternions, which adds no parameters, replaces the iterative projection with a fixed pattern of signed additions, and can be constructed faster than mHC. We evaluate oHC across a comprehensive set of downstream tasks, where it outperforms the single-stream residual baseline, mHC and iHC, which fixes the residual matrix to the identity.

</details>


### [168] [From Tokens to Semantics: Leveraging Complementary Signals for Hallucination Detection in Black-Box LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02679)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Owen O'Neill,Nabeel Kemal,Abhishek Mandal,Houssem Chatbri,Christopher Martin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在缺乏可靠上下文的情况下，基于LLM（大型语言模型）输出检测虚假内容的方法，并对多种检测手段在不同基准下的性能进行了全面比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM广泛应用于公众和高风险场景，虚假内容未检出会危害用户和机构，而误报则浪费有限的人力审核资源。尤其是在缺少可信参考的情况下，如何高效准确地检测虚假内容成为难题。

Method: 主要研究两类无监督信号：1）语义熵（基于采样响应的语义分歧）、2）由token概率推导的不确定性。提出TopK方法（汇总采样响应的token级信号）、混合的CoCoA方法（结合语义差异与不确定性）以及两种监督方法——Gated（对单一语义簇情况调用分类器）和Stacked（联合语义与token特征学习）。在七个基准上（含公开与构建数据集，涵盖文本及多模态任务），用四类模型进行测试。

Result: Stacked方法在近半数测试中表现最佳，TopK和CoCoA在无标签条件下仍具竞争力，但阈值选择需精细校准。无方法能在所有场景下始终最优。

Conclusion: 不同方法在不同场景下各有优势，需根据容忍的误报率、生成和参数校准等因素进行选取和调整，以应对多样的数据和模型特性。

Abstract: When LLMs support public-facing or high-stakes workflows, missed fabrications can harm users and institutions, while false alarms consume limited human-review capacity. When no trusted context or reference document is available, we study two signals accessible through black-box model APIs: semantic entropy, which measures disagreement among sampled response meanings, and uncertainty derived from token log-probabilities. Their failure modes can be complementary: semantic entropy becomes uninformative when responses form one semantic cluster, while token uncertainty can miss consistently confident errors. We extend token-based uncertainty detection by aggregating token-level signals across sampled responses through our TopK method, evaluate the hybrid CoCoA method, which combines target-response uncertainty with semantic dissimilarity, and propose and study two supervised methods: Gated, which routes single-cluster cases to an aggregated-token-feature classifier, and Stacked, which learns jointly from semantic uncertainty and broader token features. We evaluate seven benchmarks, including five public benchmarks (four text datasets and multimodal handwritten-cheque extraction) and two constructed benchmarks (Financial Summaries and Long-Text QA), using four language models. In our evaluation across models and datasets, Stacked gave the best performance in nearly half of the cases, while TopK and CoCoA remain competitive without supervised training labels, although their thresholds require careful calibration. No method is universally strongest. We therefore evaluate performance at false-positive-rate budgets from 1% to 15%, assess their sensitivity to generation and calibration choices, and examine variation across dataset characteristics.

</details>


### [169] [DKL: Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02685)
*Kushagra Bhushan,Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Sai Krishna Reddy Sathi,Gaurav Pandey,Sonam Gupta,Vineet Kumar,Jaydeep Sen,Yatin Nandwani,Sachindra Joshi,Dinesh Raghu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种名为DKL的新方法，用于在无需大规模指令微调的情况下，将新知识有效注入指令微调大模型（Instruct LLM），提升RAG在检索失败时的表现，并且在用更少训练数据的情况下优于以往方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法虽能利用外部知识提升大模型的事实性，但在检索出错时易出现幻觉。此前的参数注入或扩展预训练方案效率低下、成本高或会损害模型的指令跟随能力。如何在保证指令能力的同时高效注入新知识，是亟需解决的难题。

Method: 提出DKL（解耦知识学习）：不对Instruct LLM直接进行扩展预训练（EPT），而是在其基础LLM进行预训练，再通过模型权重合并的方式，将新知识注入Instruct LLM，本质上避免了昂贵的指令微调。

Result: 实验证明，DKL能将RAG在检索失败场景下的准确率从54.17提升至79.26，并以较少训练数据优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DKL是一种轻量、高效且不破坏指令能力的知识注入策略，可有效提升RAG增强大模型在知识更新和指令完成上的综合表现。

Abstract: RAG has become the de facto method for incorporating new, corpus-specific knowledge into an instruction following LLM (Instruct LLM). Although RAG-based prompting improves factual grounding, it fails when retrieval is incorrect or incomplete, leading to hallucinations. Finetuning methods such as RAFT and PA-RAG enhance RAG by injecting new knowledge into the model's parameters, but require generating a massive amount of synthetic QA that covers the entire corpus. Extended Pre-Training (EPT) on the text corpus avoids the need for comprehensive synthetic data generation but compromises an Instruct LLM's instruction-following capabilities, necessitating instruction fine-tuning (IFT) after pre-training. However, IFT is costly and may be infeasible due to the unavailability of an instruction-tuning corpus. In this work, we propose DKL-Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models. Instead of doing EPT on the Instruct LLM, DKL performs EPT on its corresponding base LLM to infuse new knowledge. These knowledge infused weights are then merged with the Instruct LLM, imparting new knowledge without affecting their instruction-following capabilities. DKL is a lightweight method that avoids expensive instruction fine-tuning and relies on model merging to infuse the new knowledge into the Instruct LLM without destroying its instruction following capabilities. Empirical results show that DKL improves RAG accuracy from 54.17 to 79.26 on retrieval failure cases, while outperforming prior approaches with substantially less training data.

</details>


### [170] [Trace as State: Reasoning Traces as Conditional States for Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02702)
*Xu Zou,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对Transformer在处理长上下文推理时存在的信息流与任务状态发现的时序错位问题，通过实验证明，在长文本前置推理痕迹有助于信息重用，显著提升模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformers天生是因果处理机制，但实际长上下文推理任务中，关键信息往往直到后文才能得知。如何解决信息流与实际推理需求的时序错位，实现高效利用先验状态信息，是提升模型长上下文理解力的核心挑战。

Method: 作者提出并分析『Trace as State』方法：在重新处理长上下文时，将先前收集的推理痕迹（作为任务状态代理）以文本形式置于输入前，而不是追加于输入后，并进行对照实验（Trace Append为后置）。

Result: 在3个模型、3个长上下文数据集上的27项模型-任务-指标组合实验中，Trace as State有26项优于Trace Append。以GraphWalks Parents数据集中DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview为例，准确率由仅用初始输入的29.2%和Trace Append的43.0%，跃升至Trace as State的81.8%；GLM-5.2模型由66.4%和83.2%提升至100%。

Conclusion: 只需调整推理痕迹的位置，将其放在长上下文之前，便可在不改变Transformer因果结构的情况下，大幅提升模型长文本推理表现。

Abstract: Transformers process information causally, but long-context reasoning may depend on task state discovered only later. We formalize this mismatch through conditional state update tasks. For causal state update processors, providing the condition first can require exponentially less memory in the worst case than providing it last.
  Motivated by this principle, we introduce Trace as State. We use collected reasoning traces as a textual proxy for task state and place it before the long-context block on a fresh pass, allowing information derived previously to guide rereading.
  We conduct extensive experiments on Trace as State and Trace Append, a matched control that uses the same task state proxy but put it after the context. Across three models and three long-context datasets, Trace as State outperforms Trace Append in 26 of 27 reported combinations of model, task, and metric. On GraphWalks Parents, exact match lifts DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview from 29.2% on the initial pass and 43.0% with Trace Appendto 81.8% with Trace as State, and from 66.4% and 83.2% to 100.0% for GLM-5.2. These results show that placing traces before the context can improve long-context reasoning while retaining the causal transformer structure.

</details>


### [171] [CORAL: An LLM-Native Harness for Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02730)
*Muhammad Rafay Azhar,Yuhang Zhou,Gilbert Jiang,Yuchen Wang,Rahul Sharma,Matthew DeSousa,Jiayi Liu,Xin Guo,Lizhu Zhang,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的推荐系统自动优化框架CORAL，可在生产环境中持续闭环优化推荐系统，无需人工干预，提升了系统的自动化和实时适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生产级推荐系统需频繁优化以适应内容和用户行为变化，但传统A/B实验依赖工程师人工干预，过程缓慢且无法全面覆盖所有系统参数，导致效率低下和响应滞后。

Method: CORAL是一个LLM原生的自动化推荐系统配置框架。框架中的智能体（agent）通过观察系统状态、回顾历史决策信息，在每个优化周期调用包括数值优化器在内的各种工具，调整推荐策略，同时确保变更受预算约束，完成闭环自主优化。优化过程不涉及模型参数更新，而是在实际环境中在线学习策略改进。

Result: 在两个大型社交平台的A/B实验中，CORAL分别实现了不增加服务成本下提升参与度，以及在不降低参与度的情况下减少服务成本，展现出在参与度-效率权衡上的优势，并且迭代越多，性能提升越明显。

Conclusion: 通过将大语言模型与自动优化闭环结合，CORAL展示了自动化持续优化推荐系统的巨大潜力，有望减少工程师手工参与，在明确约束下实现生产环境中的自适应持续优化。

Abstract: Production recommender systems shape what billions of people see, and sustaining their performance requires continual optimization: as content, user behavior, and upstream models shift, the choices governing retrieval, ranking, and serving must be revisited. Traditionally, human engineers test such changes through online experiments--a slow, reactive process limited by engineering effort, leaving parts of the system unrevised as conditions change. Although large language models have been applied to ranking, user modeling, and offline model development, few systems place an agent in a continual closed loop that acts on a live recommender and learns from the measured effects of its decisions. We present CORAL (Constraint-Optimized Recommender via an Agentic Loop), an LLM-native harness that closes this loop: each cycle, the agent observes operating signals, reasons over a memory of past decisions and outcomes, and invokes tools--including a numerical optimizer that keeps changes within a fixed operating budget--to reconfigure the recommender, with measured outcomes informing the next cycle. We formulate this as a partially observed, non-stationary, constrained optimization problem in which the policy improves in context, without parameter updates, from its prior actions. Across two large-scale social platforms, evaluated with A/B experiments, the same harness improves engagement at no additional serving cost on one and reduces serving cost without degrading engagement on the other, spanning the engagement-efficiency frontier. Performance improves as the loop iterates, suggesting that a single agentic loop can automate continual optimization work traditionally performed by human algorithm engineers under explicit guardrails.

</details>


### [172] [Choosing a PEFT Variant for Per-Patient Dysarthric ASR: A Single-Speaker Case Study on Two ASR Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02735)
*Bernard Muller,László Tóth,LaVonne Roberts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了七种LoRA系列的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法在重度言语障碍（dysarthria）患者的个体自适应ASR中的表现。结果发现LoRA表现最佳，并在存储效率上有突出优势，部分方法显著提升字符错误率（CER），但完整微调依旧最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前面向严重言语障碍患者（如中风后dysarthria）的ASR系统，普遍依赖每位患者单独适配的adapter架构，然而针对该情景下各PEFT方法优劣尚无系统性对比。本工作旨在明确不同PEFT方法在极端个体化、自适应ASR场景下的实际效果和效率。

Method: 作者针对一位重度dysarthria的匈牙利语男性患者，比较了七种LoRA家族的PEFT方法，包括LoRA、QLoRA、AdaLoRA、DoRA、LoHA、VeRA和VB-LoRA，并在两个主流ASR基础模型上进行实验（Whisper-large-v3及Qwen3-ASR-1.7B）。评估指标为字符错误率（CER），采用bootstrap统计测试显著性。

Result: Attention-projection适配器大幅降低了CER。LoRA与DoRA在统计上无显著区别，且LoRA实现更为简单高效。QLoRA在CER和存储上均表现较差，LoHA等其余方法尽管有改进，但未超越LoRA系列。完整微调CER最低，但LoRA几乎能以远低于存储代价获得相近表现。只需约5分钟语音，即可获得近一半定制化提升。

Conclusion: LoRA方法在自适应、个体化ASR任务中兼顾高效性与性能，适合大规模临床落地。当前结果基于单例研究，需更多受试者验证。研究为临床dysarthria ASR系统的高效适配提供了理论和实证基础。

Abstract: Per-patient adapters are the preferred production architecture for dysarthric automatic speech recognition (ASR), yet parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) variants have not been compared in the speaker-dependent, per-patient regime. We present a single-speaker case study comparing seven LoRA-family methods (LoRA, QLoRA, AdaLoRA, DoRA, LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA) on two production bases (Whisper-large-v3 with Hungarian fine-tuning, and a multilingual Qwen3-ASR-1.7B checkpoint) for one post-stroke Hungarian male speaker (S1, 409 utterances; severe dysarthria on auditory-perceptual clinical assessment). Attention-projection adapters substantially improve CER on both bases. Across three seeds, a paired bootstrap detects no significant LoRA-DoRA difference (p>0.5; 13.86/13.90 % CER on Whisper, 28.10/28.33 % on Qwen3-ASR), so we adopt the simpler, cheaper LoRA. Real 4-bit (NF4) QLoRA is worse on every seed and both bases (14.56/30.09 % CER) with no memory saving at this scale, and LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA and AdaLoRA do not reach the LoRA family, though LoHA still gives an 18.6 % relative CER reduction on Whisper. On the same base, full fine-tuning is more accurate (11.43 % CER), but a 115 MB LoRA that also adapts the feed-forward blocks reaches within 0.66 pp of it at approximately 3.7 % of the per-patient storage. A 6-point enrollment grid shows about 5 min of patient audio captures 45.6 % of the zero-shot-to-30-min CER reduction, with further gains at 10 and 30 min (caveat: one speaker, one language, severe post-stroke dysarthria). Training scripts and recipes will be released, source-available under a research-use licence, on publication.

</details>


### [173] [Language Models Can Control Their Own Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02737)
*Namgyu Ho,Huzama Ahmad,Woosung Koh,Se-Young Yun,Tal Schuster,Cicero Nogueira dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种叫做Declarative Attention（DA）的新注意力机制，通过让语言模型明确声明需要关注的上下文区域，有效减少了长上下文推理时需要读取的KV缓存，提高了推理效率，且只带来了极小的准确率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文下，现有模型在解码时需要每步都遍历整个KV缓存（全上下文），不必要地浪费计算资源。主流做法虽然引入代理分数预选相关token，但依然在每步存在O(N)的复杂度。作者希望通过让模型自身判断需要关注的部分，实现更高效的上下文筛选。

Method: 提出Declarative Attention（DA）协议，将生成过程分为三种声明模式：<global>（全量）、<focus>（区域）、<local>（仅看最近内容）。模型在生成时主动声明关注范围，推理引擎解析声明，实现跳读KV缓存，大幅降低需要访问的token数。

Result: 在Gemma-4-31B、Qwen-3.6-27B等主流大模型上进行15项长上下文任务的零样本评测，DA方法分别减少了52.0%和31.1%的平均解码时访问token数量，准确率仅小幅下降（1.27和2.75个百分点），且随着模型规模增加损失趋小。

Conclusion: DA为实现稀疏注意力带来了全新维度，无需训练即可显著提升效率，且保持高准确率，对后续研究和基于训练的进一步提升有很大潜力。

Abstract: Language models spend most of their attention on a small fraction of context, yet they read the entire KV cache to find the few tokens that matter. If the user asks about a previous detail in a 1M-token conversation, global attention layers must scan the full context to generate each token of the reply. A prominent approach mitigates this cost by pre-selecting relevant tokens via lightweight proxy scores, but this extrinsic scoring still incurs O(N) per step. We take an intrinsic approach motivated by the simple question: wouldn't the model already know which parts of the context are relevant? To this end, we introduce Declarative Attention (DA), a protocol that elicits the model to declare where it needs to attend within its chain-of-thought, partitioning generation into three modes: <global> (full context), <focus> (a specific region), and <local> (recent output only). The inference engine parses these declarations like tool calls and skips most of the KV cache read. Under zero-shot evaluation across 15 long-context tasks, DA on off-the-shelf models (Gemma-4-31B, Qwen-3.6-27B) significantly reduces total attended tokens during decoding (52.0%, 31.1%) with modest accuracy drops (1.27pp, 2.75pp) that shrink with model scale. DA unlocks a new axis of sparse attention, with further potential under training-based methods that future work can explore.

</details>


### [174] [Untangling the Mechanisms of Misleading Context in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02754)
*Robin Linzmayer,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文探讨了大型语言模型在医学问答中对误导性上下文的易感性及其影响，并评估了检测和监控误导决策的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在医学问答领域达到专家水平，其处理的上下文有可能带有误导信息，这种误导会腐蚀模型的医学判断。因此，理解模型是如何被误导性信息影响、以及如何检测此类影响变得尤为重要。

Method: 作者使用了经临床专家审查的MedMisBench数据集，通过注入两类误导性上下文（虚构证据与直接断言），对三种推理模型（两种披露推理过程，一种只披露答案）进行实验，分析模型对误导的反应、披露情况、推理过程中的机理及监控的有效性。

Result: 三种模型对直接断言比虚构证据更易受影响，采用误导答案的概率高出10-27个百分点。模型在开放推理痕迹下误导信息披露率为81-98%，仅回答时为7-90%。误导证据通常早期进入推理并逐步积累，而断言则在结论形成时起到关键作用。利用开放推理轨迹和指导的LLM监控能够以5%误报率检测78%被误导的决策，单看答案最多只能检测32%。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型对直接断言类误导信息尤其敏感且此类误导披露率低，只有在完全开放推理过程时监控机制才能可靠检测，然而这类推理往往被前沿模型提供商所隐藏。

Abstract: Large language models now answer medical questions with expert-level performance. However, the context these systems act on can be misleading, and misleading context can corrupt a model's medical judgment. To understand how misleading context corrupts this judgment, we examine the model's susceptibility to the context, disclosure of it, mechanism of corrupted reasoning, and monitorability of the decision. On the medical reasoning subset of MedMisBench, a clinician-reviewed question-answering benchmark of 8,627 questions, we inject two types of misleading context cues, fabricated evidence and a bare assertion. We test three reasoning models, two that expose their full reasoning trace and one frontier model that exposes only its response. All three are more susceptible to the assertion than to the fabricated evidence, adopting the asserted answer 10 to 27 points more often. The misleading cues are disclosed in 81 to 98% of traces but only 7 to 90% of responses, and the assertion is disclosed less often than evidence based cues. Resampling from reasoning traces without disclosure shows the two cues corrupt reasoning differently, evidence entering early and accumulating while the assertion redirects the conclusion near its end. An LLM monitor catches 78% of corrupted decisions at 5% false positives when reading an open model's trace with guidance, against at most 32% from any response. The misleading context that models are most susceptible to is disclosed least, and was caught reliably only from an open reasoning trace, which frontier providers withhold.

</details>


### [175] [From Reweighting to Rewriting: Unlocking the Intervention Effects of Influential Samples in Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02771)
*Yuzhang Luo,Chenpeng Wang,Jianhui Chen,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了训练数据归因(TDA)对大模型行为干预的实际效果，发现传统的基于权重重分配的干预有限，将IF指导下样本内容重写方法可显著提升干预效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管影响函数(IF)可用于识别影响力样本，但用IF选择样本随后通过常规重加权干预，却未能表现出显著优于随机选择。研究动机在于深究问题根源——是样本本身缺乏干预价值，还是重加权方式难以发挥所选样本的潜力。

Method: 提出"IF指导下的响应重写"：利用IF选择关键训练样本，并将其输出答案替换为期望行为（正向或反向），而不改变原始指令，以此与传统的权重重分配方法在相同数据集上做对比。测试平台为知识不确定（epistemic abstention）场景，并辅以安全拒答等对比实验。

Result: 结果显示：采用响应重写方法对模型行为的调整更强、更持久且支持双向干预，而权重重分配仅带来微弱且不稳定效果。此外，利用IF选中的样本相较其他选择方法，在重写操作下能引发更大且更集中于目标行为的变化。

Conclusion: 影响函数所体现的本地权重重分配效果与其识别样本的全局干预杠杆间存在显著差异。应关注数据归因方法在干预中的实际效果，未来TDA方法评估需结合与干预关联的效果指标。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) aims to identify training examples that shape model behavior, but its intervention value depends on both which examples are selected and how they are modified. Influence functions (IF) estimate behavioral changes under infinitesimal reweighting, yet IF-selected examples often show limited advantages over random selection under conventional weight-based interventions. This raises the question of whether influential examples lack intervention value or whether reweighting fails to realize their behavioral leverage.We introduce influence-guided response rewriting, which uses IF to identify intervention targets and replaces their responses with behavior-aligned or behavior-opposed supervision while keeping instructions fixed. Across four open-weight LLMs, we compare rewriting and reweighting on the same influence-selected examples using epistemic abstention as our primary testbed. Response rewriting produces stronger, more persistent, and bidirectional behavioral shifts, while reweighting the same examples yields weak and inconsistent effects. Further analyses show that influence-selected examples provide greater rewriting leverage than alternative selectors, with changes remaining concentrated on target-relevant behaviors. The same qualitative contrast extends to safety refusal. These results distinguish the local reweighting effects captured by influence estimates from the broader intervention leverage of the examples they identify, motivating intervention-aware evaluation of TDA methods.

</details>


### [176] [HyperStyler: Low-resource Authorship Style Transfer via Context-aware Style Navigation and Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02772)
*Jongkyung Shin,Minguk Jeon,Chanwoo Park,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向低资源作者风格迁移的新方法HyperStyler，将风格迁移分为风格选择和风格实现两个阶段，效果优于现有方法，并且高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低资源作者风格迁移方法难以同时保证风格一致性和语义保持，主要因为其风格建模方式存在信息丢失和混淆问题。

Method: 提出了HyperStyler架构，通过风格选择模块（Stylo-navigator）结合源文本与目标作者参考预测风格坐标，再由风格实现模块（Stylo-hypernet）通过动态参数调整实现风格迁移，避免风格和内容特征混淆。

Result: 在Reddit、Blog和News数据集上的实验显示，HyperStyler在风格一致性和语义保持方面显著优于以前的方法，包括基于大模型的方案，同时推理速度快，仅比T5-large多2.4%的参数，推理速度为LLM的1.8倍。

Conclusion: HyperStyler有效解决了低资源风格迁移中的主要难点，实现了更好的风格和语义分离与操控，且具有高效和可扩展的优势。

Abstract: Low-resource authorship style transfer (LAST) aims to rewrite text into the style of an arbitrary target author using only a few reference examples while preserving the original meaning. Existing methods often struggle to achieve both high style fidelity and semantic preservation because they compress diverse references into a single static author embedding, which averages out context-dependent stylistic variation, and rely on hidden representations for style control, which entangle style with content. We propose HyperStyler, a novel architecture that decouples LAST into style selection and style realization. Stylo-navigator predicts style coordinates by jointly modeling the source context and target-author references, and Stylo-hypernet realizes them via dynamic parameter modulation instead of hidden-state injection. Our experiments on Reddit, Blog, and News datasets demonstrate that HyperStyler consistently outperforms prior methods including LLM-based approaches and generalizes robustly across domains. Notably, HyperStyler achieves superior performance with as few as 2.4% additional parameters over T5-large, while being over 1.8x faster than LLMs at inference.

</details>


### [177] [EarlyEval: Cheaper Agent Evaluation via Early Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02783)
*Yuling Shi,Zhensu Sun,Junsen Dong,Chengcheng Wan,David Lo,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EarlyEval框架，通过在任务执行过程中预测代理结果，提前终止推理过程，从而显著降低大模型代理评测成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理的评测成本极高，一次完整测评耗资巨大，并且随开发迭代不断重复，而现有的任务精简方法无法降低单个任务评测时的费用。

Method: 作者提出了一种“早期结果预测”方法，利用LightGBM模型对中间行为、文本和参考解特征进行分类预测，在达到高置信度后提前终止任务执行。具体方法为在SWE-bench Verified、TerminalBench、Toolathlon等基准上实时预测成功或失败，并立即结束后续步骤。

Result: EarlyEval能减少13%-26%的代理推理步数，最多降低44.1%的输入token和29.4%的输出token消耗，预测准确率89%-97%，对任务解决率影响极小（1-2个百分点）。

Conclusion: EarlyEval是一种高效轻量的评测加速工具，可显著压缩评测成本，在保持高准确率的同时提升开发效率。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents is essential for guiding their development, yet it has grown prohibitively expensive: a single pass of a frontier model over an agentic benchmark can cost hundreds to thousands of dollars, a price paid repeatedly across iterative development cycles. Prior efforts, centered on benchmark distillation, reduce the number of evaluation tasks but leave the cost of executing each retained task untouched. In this work, we introduce early outcome prediction, a complementary axis of efficiency that instead cuts cost within each task. Our key insight is that an agent's final outcome is often evident from its intermediate behavior well before execution completes. We instantiate this idea in EarlyEval, a lightweight framework that trains a pair of LightGBM success and failure classifiers over behavioral, textual, and reference-solution features, and halts an agent run the moment either classifier crosses a calibrated confidence threshold, adding negligible per-step overhead. Across three benchmarks, SWE-bench Verified, TerminalBench, and Toolathlon, EarlyEval can eliminate 13%-26% of agent steps and up to 44.1% input tokens and 29.4% output tokens at 89%-97% prediction accuracy, while perturbing per-agent resolve rates by only one to two percentage points on average.

</details>


### [178] [DiscoSign: Discourse-Aware Text to Sign Language Gloss Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02796)
*Vasileios Baltatzis,Mert Inan,Connor Gillis,Raja Kushalnagar,Lorna Quandt,Leah Findlater,Colin Lea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiscoSign，一个面向话语层面（discourse-aware）的文本到手语字面（gloss）翻译系统，能够更好地处理手语翻译中的篇章一致性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手语处理系统通常只处理单句翻译，忽视了手语理解和使用中关键的话语现象（如篇章一致性与实体位置跟踪），导致翻译质量有限。

Method: 本文基于大型语言模型（LLM），构建了模块化的翻译框架，重点解决三个问题：1）空间共指消解（即实体在话语中的空间位置一致性）；2）疑问-答案从句结构（QAC）；3）概念和手语字面的一致性。同时提出了一套新的评价指标，用以检测话语一致性方面的提升。

Result: 实验表明，该系统在句子级和话语级数据集上均可显著提升空间一致性和实体追踪能力，同时保持与现有方法相当的单句翻译质量。

Conclusion: 本文首次提出了系统化的话语级文本到手语字面翻译框架，并配套了相应的评价方法，对提升手语自动翻译系统的话语理解能力具有重要意义。

Abstract: Sign language processing systems have traditionally operated at the sentence level, ignoring critical discourse phenomena fundamental to sign language comprehension. We introduce DiscoSign, a computational approach for discourse-aware text to sign language gloss translation grounded in linguistic research. We address three key phenomena within our modular Large Language Model (LLM)-based translation framework: (i) spatial coreference resolution, where entities maintain consistent spatial locations throughout discourse; (ii) Question-Answer Clauses (QACs), pseudocleft structures serving specific discourse functions; and (iii) concept-gloss consistency, ensuring stable mappings between English concepts and American Sign Language (ASL) signs. Traditional translation metrics fail to capture discourse-level quality, so we introduce a suite of novel evaluation metrics designed to assess each dimension of discourse coherence addressed by our framework. Experiments on sentence-level and discourse-level datasets show that our approach for discourse-aware processing significantly improves spatial consistency and entity tracking relative to sentence-only translation, while maintaining competitive single-sentence gloss translation quality. Our work establishes the first systematic framework for discourse-level text to sign language gloss translation with corresponding evaluation methodology.

</details>


### [179] [User Feedback Provides a Unique Signal that LLMs Can not Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02859)
*Shachar Don-Yehiya,Leshem Choshen,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现用户交互反馈对于提升大语言模型能力极为有用，但当前评估方法存在系统性偏差掩盖了其有效性。作者通过合成数据和真实数据验证，证明反馈引导的模型修正明显优于未利用反馈的修正。此外，揭示了现有LLM评测往往不能识别这些真实改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去研究认为用户反馈嘈杂且难以利用，导致对其价值的低估。作者想挑战这一观点，澄清反馈的实际作用及现有评价体系的不足。

Method: 作者设计了两套数据集：一套包含明确标准答案的合成数据，另一套是反映真实场景的自然数据。在两种数据上对比有无用户反馈的模型修正效果，并以LLM自动评价结果分析评测偏差来源。

Result: 实验显示，利用反馈后的模型修正能更高效地解决目标问题，胜率远超未用反馈的基线修正。但当前由LLM自动评审的系统，常常无法正确识别这些改进，甚至倾向于选择较差的答案。

Conclusion: 用户反馈是提升大模型的有效信号，其价值被评价体系系统性低估。提升评估方法能大幅改善模型演化效率。

Abstract: Harnessing naturally occurring feedback from user interactions offers a promising learning signal for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies suggest this feedback is inherently noisy and difficult to leverage effectively. We challenge this conception by demonstrating that user feedback is a highly actionable signal for improvement, and that its perceived ineffectiveness stems from a systematic bias in current evaluation paradigms. To isolate the usefulness of feedback, we construct synthetic data with a definitive ground truth, alongside naturalistic data to validate that our findings hold in real-world scenarios. By comparing model revisions generated with and without access to feedback across both settings, we show that feedback-informed revisions resolve targeted issues at significantly higher rates than baseline revisions. Finally, we expose the root of the evaluation bias: when a model successfully fixes an issue exclusively due to feedback, LLM judges frequently fail to identify the genuinely corrected response, systematically preferring inferior baseline outputs instead.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [Not All Agreement Counts as Corroboration: Provenance-Conserving Multi-View Fusion for Typed Action Admission in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01662)
*Zekai Jin,Hanrong Zhang,Yihong Tang,Fei Hu,Zhen Dong,Yi Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 单靠预测一致性无法决定是否采取行动，证据的来源同样关键。PACT方法通过保留证据来源，确保只有独立来源的证据才被累加，提高了证据聚合的可靠性。实验表明该方法有效降低了风险并提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的体感系统在采取行动时，多数仅依赖预测结果的一致性，但没有考虑证据是否来自相互独立的来源。重复利用同一观测可能导致一致性被放大，却没有实际增加证据强度，这会影响决策的可靠性。因此，研究动机是将证据的可计数性和来源信息引入融合决策过程，增强模型对来源多样性的辨识和利用能力。

Method: 提出了PACT（Provenance-Aware Countable Tallying）方法，通过对每个证据单元指定来源分区，对其信息进行保源融合（provenance-conserving fusion），仅在独立来源之间进行证据累积，并设置释放条件（hold, confirm, fallback）以调节最终行动的触发。理论上证明该方法满足单例忠实性和不会人为放大证据量。

Result: 在包含31,200次评估的48个场景簇中，PACT的归一化风险覆盖指标(ncsAURC)达0.0861。与单例聚合相比，来源分区聚合平均减少ncsAURC 0.0557，且在排除对抗性场景后，证据对比优势消失。在完整来源记录下，PACT在原生成得分上表现更优，但在统一评分下与其他方法差距缩小。此外，分配不同来源不会改变预测，但会按照预期调整证据预算。机器人合作实验表明，分组PACT方法无误将参考一致候选纳入，无参考不一致案例。

Conclusion: PACT系统能够有效区分独立与非独立的证据来源，并只在合适的前提下累加证据，显著提升了证据融合的可靠性与安全性。实验证明方法在多场景下均有稳健表现，推荐用于需要严谨证据判别的体感或机器人应用。

Abstract: For embodied systems, predictive agreement alone does not determine whether evidence warrants action; evidential origin matters. Repeated inference over one observation can multiply agreement without adding evidence, while source-local values do not reveal whether outputs have separately countable origins. PACT treats evidence countability as a relational variable for provenance-conserving fusion and typed action admission. A supplied provenance partition defines countable units. PACT retains coordinatewise support shared within each unit, accumulates only across units, and maps unmet release conditions to hold, confirm, or fallback. Under the stated assumptions, source-local values cannot identify countability; the coordinatewise meet is the greatest budget satisfying singleton fidelity and insertion non-amplification, with coarsening monotonicity and fixed-partition stability. Across 31,200 evaluations in 48 scene clusters, PACT attains a common-support normalized risk-coverage area (ncsAURC) of 0.0861. Excluding the constructed adversarial-consensus arm, provenance-partition aggregation reduces ncsAURC by 0.0557 relative to singleton aggregation, while the corroboration contrast vanishes. On complete-source records, native scores favor PACT, but a common posterior-peak score narrows its difference from nested Dirichlet and favors product fusion. Reassigning provenance over unchanged predictions moves evidence budgets as predicted. In offline human-robot collaboration, eightfold within-camera duplication leaves 720 typed responses per checkpoint unchanged; camera-grouped PACT admits 47 of 57 Qwen3-VL-32B reference-consistent candidates with no observed reference-inconsistent admission in 60 episodes. PACT separates computational from evidential multiplicity: agreement constitutes corroboration only when provenance permits separate accumulation.

</details>


### [181] [TriSAR: Task Coordination and Collision Avoidance for Aerial Robot Teams in Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01731)
*Aditya Anil Kapile,Pedro Machado,Isibor Kennedy Ihianle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过实验比较了无人机团队协作中不同任务分配和避障策略对任务效率与安全性能的影响，发现任务分配和避障方式的选择对安全和效率具有显著且相互依赖的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多无人机协同应急响应系统需要高效的任务分配与实时避障协调，但两者单独或联合对执行效率和安全性的具体影响缺乏系统分析。该文旨在通过严格实验分析，量化两种常见任务分配方法结合或不结合避障机制的实际效果。

Method: 在Gazebo物理仿真平台中搭建地震后的城市场景，采用5架无人机，针对8个目标，分别实现遗传算法与贪婪式任务分配，各自配合或不配合反应式避障。通过2x2因子实验设计，每种组合模拟30次，对碰撞违例和各任务效率指标进行统计检验。

Result: 贪婪分配下，只要使用反应式避障，所有碰撞门限违例被消除（Mann-Whitney检验显著）。GA分配下同样得到安全提升，但效果较弱。在效率指标上，如启用避障，GA分配效率无显著提升；若禁用避障，GA分配在步数、路径长度与能耗等方面明显优于贪婪分配。

Conclusion: 反应式避障能显著提升机群安全性，且效果依赖于任务分配策略。遗传算法虽计算量大，但其效率提升只在未加避障时才有显著体现。为兼顾安全与效率，应根据实际需求合理选择协调策略。

Abstract: Multi-Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) disaster-response systems require coordinated task assignment and local trajectory control, yet the individual and combined contributions of these coordination layers to mission efficiency and operational safety remain insufficiently characterised under controlled experimental conditions. TriSAR is evaluated as a five-UAV coordination system operating in a physics-based Gazebo simulation of an earthquake-damaged urban environment. A 2 x 2 factorial design compares two task-allocation strategies (Genetic Algorithm and greedy fitness-based allocation) with reactive collision avoidance enabled or disabled. Each of the four configurations was evaluated over 30 stochastic episodes in a common scenario of five UAVs and eight targets. Under greedy allocation, enabling repulsion eliminated recorded collision-threshold violations, confirmed by a Mann-Whitney test (U = 885, p = 4.03 x 10^-12, rank-biserial r = 0.97). Under GA allocation, the same protective effect was confirmed (U = 675, p = 1.26 x 10^-5, rank-biserial r = 0.50). For mission-efficiency metrics, GA-based allocation showed no statistically detectable advantage over greedy allocation when repulsion was enabled, but a significant advantage in steps, path length, and energy when repulsion was disabled (Welch's t-tests, |g| between 0.92 and 1.76). These results show that reactive repulsion provides a substantial, allocation-dependent safety benefit, while the additional computational complexity of GA-based task allocation yields a detectable mission-efficiency benefit only when repulsion is disabled.

</details>


### [182] [Designing Versatile Samples for Learned Trajectory Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01799)
*Yaguang Li,Jiaru Zhang,Chuheng Wei,Can Cui,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过设计更具信息量的正负样本数据集，提升自动驾驶决策过程中轨迹评分器（scorer）的训练效果，使其在选择候选轨迹时更加智能和鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法通常将轨迹生成与选择解耦，评分器可以独立训练，但候选轨迹大多集中在安全区域，难以涵盖决策边界，限制了评分器对复杂决策的学习。

Method: 作者构建了两个针对车辆可移动方向（横向靠近道路边界和纵向靠近前车）的扰动生成器，从记录的人类驾驶轨迹生成更具挑战性的训练数据，并将Transformer架构的评分器连接到冻结的两种生成性规划器（DiffusionDrive和MeanFuser）上进行训练。

Result: 在NAVSIM navtrain数据集上进行实验，评分器基于ResNet-34时，在DiffusionDrive和MeanFuser下分别达到90.1与90.4 EPDMS，且使用设计的数据集时EDPMS分别提升0.4和0.3。

Conclusion: 通过设计更加信息丰富的训练数据全面提升了轨迹评分器的性能，使其在现实场景决策边界附近具有更强判别与泛化能力，推动端到端自动驾驶决策体系进步。

Abstract: Many current end-to-end driving policies emit a pool of candidate trajectories and select one, which makes selection a separable component: a scorer can be retrained while the planner, its backbone, and its trajectory generator all stay frozen. However, many strong planners concentrate their proposals around safe mode, providing limited supervision near decision boundaries. In this work, we design a training dataset that provides more informative supervision for the scorer. In particular, we construct two generators that perturb the logged human trajectory along the two axes a vehicle can be displaced: laterally toward the drivable boundary and longitudinally toward a leading vehicle. The designed dataset produces more informative positive and negative samples than the base planner's proposal pool. We attach a transformer-based scorer to two frozen generative planners, DiffusionDrive and MeanFuser, and train it on the NAVSIM navtrain dataset. The results of the experiments show that we achieve 90.1 EPDMS on DiffusionDrive and 90.4 EPDMS on MeanFuser when using ResNet-34, with 0.4 and 0.3 EPDMS respectively, from the designed training dataset.

</details>


### [183] [One Demonstration, Many Objects: Generalizing Manipulation via Local Contact Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01938)
*Satvik Sharma,Samrat Sahoo,Huang Huang,Fei-Fei Li Jiajun Wu,Dorsa Sadigh,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的灵巧机械手对象操作策略DemoMimic，通过强调接触点的局部几何来提升类人操控能力，实现了跨多对象和任务的高效sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧机械手有实现类人精细操作的潜力，但大规模真实数据收集困难；现有利用人类示范作为先验的方法存在奖励设计不精和泛化性差的问题，亟需新的方法提升实际应用表现。

Method: 提出DemoMimic策略，核心是基于接触点局部几何，以接触为中心设计奖励函数，引导策略学会精准接触，实现对多形状、尺寸、质量、摩擦系数对象的通用操控能力。

Result: DemoMimic在16个物体、4项任务和2种机械手平台上实现71%成功率，ablation实验显示sim-to-real性能损失最小，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: DemoMimic通过关注接触几何特征与奖励设计，有效提升了灵巧机械手的实物操作能力和跨对象泛化能力，为类人操控与现实应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered robot hands promises human-level dexterity, but collecting large-scale dexterous robot hand data remains difficult. Learning from human demonstrations has emerged as a scalable alternative to robot teleoperation, providing strong priors on object interaction and contact strategies. Recent sim-to-real RL methods incorporate such priors, but often (i) omit rewards that explicitly incentivize precise contact, yielding weak real-world performance, and/or (ii) generalize poorly to unseen object instances. We propose DemoMimic (Dexterous Motion Mimic), a policy that manipulates objects by focusing on their geometry local to the contact points. Its contact-centric rewards encourage precise contact and improve sim-to-real consistency, yielding a single real-world policy that transfers across objects of varying shape, scale, mass, and friction wherever local contact structure is preserved. Real-world ablations show that DemoMimic achieves 71% success across 16 objects, four tasks, and two robot-hand embodiments, with the smallest sim-to-real drop compared to baselines.

</details>


### [184] [MACAW: Reliable And Efficient Surgical Debridement Using Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01961)
*Ziyang Chen,Shutong Jin,Preethi Satish,Sareena Mann,Cael Magner,Danyal Fer,Omid Mohareri,Gary Guthart,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一套增强手术机器人灵巧度的系统，通过视觉伺服和新颖的深度控制方法MACAW，显著提升了外科去除坏死组织（debridement）的效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科手术中，医生常常受到空间感知不精确和机械操作局限的影响，进行如坏死组织清除（debridement）等任务时效率低、负担重。提升机器人的操作灵巧性，有望让医生摆脱繁琐、重复的子任务。

Method: 设计了一套增强型灵巧度系统，利用视觉伺服技术精确对准夹持器与手术目标；提出了一种新型深度控制方法MACAW（单目自适应紧凑注意力窗口）；系统在da Vinci手术机器人上验证，并对比过程式和VLA深度估计等基线方法。

Result: 在100次物理实验中，相机伺服将夹持器位置误差由37像素优化到5像素以内，平均仅耗时0.39秒。MACAW方法去除碎片的成功率达93%，单个碎片清除用时11秒，小时处理量为304件。扩展到双手操作，成功率为92%，平均每片7秒，小时处理量提升至473件。

Conclusion: 提出的增强灵巧度系统配合MACAW方法显著提高了机器人执行debridement的效率与成功率，有望减轻外科医生的繁重操作并拓展手术机器人应用价值。

Abstract: Augmenting the dexterity of human surgeons has the potential to free them from tedious subtasks. We consider debridement (removal of diseased or dead tissue fragments), which is challenging due to imprecision in spatial perception and cable actuation. We develop an augmented dexterity system for surgical debridement that uses visual servoing to align the cable-driven gripper with the target position in the image plane, and then introduces a novel approach to depth control, MACAW: Monocular Adaptive Compact Attention Windows. Across 100 physical trials using the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) robot, camera-frame servoing reduced average gripper position offset from 37 to fewer than 5 pixels within 4 optimization steps, taking an average of only 0.39s. MACAW significantly outperforms procedural and learned VLA baselines, achieving a 93% success rate at 11 seconds per fragment, yielding a throughput of 304 fragments per hour. Extending MACAW to a bimanual debridement setup maintains a 92% success rate at an average of 7 seconds per fragment, increasing the throughput to 473 fragments per hour.

</details>


### [185] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight, Low-Profile Powered Knee Prosthesis with Quasi-Direct Drive Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02003)
*Ross J. Cortino,Ryan Posh,Emily G. Keller,Robert D. Gregg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种新型轻量化、低轮廓的QDD膝关节假肢，具高输出力矩、精确控制和优越动力学性能，临床实际可行性佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全主动膝关节假肢虽具优势，但QDD（准直接驱动）执行器过重、过大，阻碍商业推广。需减少体积和重量，同时维持高性能。

Method: 设计并优化18:1两级传动结构，结合热与结构有限元分析减重，制成2.6kg、24.5cm高度假肢，并通过台架和用户实验测试输出力矩、抗回驱扭矩及生物力学表现。

Result: 实现了145Nm峰值力矩、1Nm低回驱扭矩，具精确的位置和力矩控制。实验中，双腿截肢者在行走与坐站转换时，假肢能实现生物仿真的动力学参数，并达到健常人扩展力矩的标准差范围。

Conclusion: 该轻量化QDD膝关节假肢达到或优于主流商品假肢的质量、尺寸、峰值力矩和噪音要求，显示出QDD假肢在临床动态性能改善和实际可行性方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Fully-powered knee prostheses, unlike traditional passive knees, can perform controlled positive work, reducing the need for compensatory behaviors by users during energy-intensive activities. While quasi-direct drive (QDD) actuators provide superior torque control, backdrivability, and acoustic noise properties compared to traditional highly-geared actuators, prior QDD prototypes have been too heavy and bulky for commercial translation. In this work, we present the design and validation of a new lightweight (2.6 kg) and low-profile (24.5 cm tip-to-tip build height) QDD knee prosthesis. By optimizing an 18 to 1 two-stage transmission alongside thermal and structural finite-element analyses, we significantly reduce device mass while enabling a peak torque of 145 Nm. Through benchtop tests, we validate the device's high output torque, low backdrive torque (1 Nm), and its precision position and torque control capabilities. We also demonstrate biomimetic kinematics and peak knee extension torques (within one standard deviation of able-bodied references) during both level-ground walking and sit-stand transitions performed by three participants with transfemoral amputation and varying K-levels. By meeting or improving upon the mass, build height, peak torque, and acoustic noise of a leading commercial powered knee, this work establishes the clinical viability of emerging QDD prostheses that promise improved dynamic performance for their users.

</details>


### [186] [Real-Time Dynamics-Based Torque-Sampling MPPI for Compliant and Force Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02020)
*Euncheol Im,Taehyun Kim,Yonghwan Oh,Myotaeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MPPI的机器人操作臂任务空间控制新框架，可高效实时解决非线性动力学并实现安全与顺应性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对机器人在非结构化环境中与人或物体的安全物理交互需求，现有MPC方法对非线性刚体动力学的实时求解能力有限，难以有效实现顺应性和力控制。

Method: 引入Model Predictive Path Integral（MPPI）方法，在实时MPC架构中显式求解刚体动力学，并通过力矩采样和GPU并行化提升计算效率，确保系统对非线性动态、物理约束的快速决策能力。

Result: 该方法在7自由度机械臂上进行实验证实：在0.18秒预测视野下，求解频率超过166Hz，顺应性与力控制表现优秀。

Conclusion: 新框架可使机器人操作臂在复杂非结构化环境中安全、顺应和高效地完成物理交互，显示出对非线性任务空间控制的实时优越性。

Abstract: This study proposes a novel Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI)-based task-space control framework. The proposed framework explicitly solves rigid-body dynamics within a real-time MPC formulation and enforces safety constraints, enabling accurate motion and force control that yields compliant behaviors for safe and effective physical interaction of robotic manipulators in unstructured environments. By leveraging MPPI, the proposed framework efficiently handles nonlinear dynamics that are difficult to solve with conventional MPC approaches in real-time. Furthermore, we develop a torque-sampling-based control architecture that enables efficient exploitation of GPU-based parallelization, resulting in effective compliant and force-aware behaviors. As a result, the proposed framework achieves a solver update rate of over 166 Hz with a 0.18 s prediction horizon, and its performance is validated through real-world experiments on a 7-DoF manipulator.

</details>


### [187] [Modeling What Changes: Sparse, Residual World Models for Object-Centric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02046)
*Param Thakkar,Parsika Paresh Shah,Manisha Sushant Gote*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稀疏残差世界模型，重点只预测对象发生变化的部分，大幅提升预测准确率，并大幅降低参数量。通过多种实验，模型在预测精度、参数效率、泛化能力以及在规划任务中的表现均优于传统的整体预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型在每一步都需预测整个场景的下一个状态，导致对静态部分资源浪费且引入误差。因此，研究者希望找到一种更有效、可解释的物理预测与控制建模方式。

Method: 作者设计了一种以对象为中心的稀疏/残差模型，用“变化门控”决定哪些对象将发生变化，并为这些对象输出增量变化，仅对变化部分建模，避免重复预测静态部分。并在MuJoCo桌面推物环境中与传统多层感知机（MLP）进行对比实验。

Result: 该方法在对象数量从3到8的场景下，比密集MLP模型准确2.5～4.6倍，参数量仅为1/8到1/11。变化检测F1高达0.80~0.87，泛化到不同对象数时仍保持99.4%的F1且无需重新训练，且使用更少数据即可得到高准确率。在自回归推理和规划应用中也表现出更优误差控制和实际效能。

Conclusion: 只建模发生变化的对象是面向对象物理智能建模中的一种简洁有效偏置。该思路带来更有效、更具解释性、更具泛化能力的预测与控制能力，有望推动物理世界AI的发展。源码等资源将随论文公开。

Abstract: Monolithic world models predict the entire next state at every step, spending capacity re-predicting the static majority of a scene and injecting error into it. We ask whether explicitly modeling change (a per-object change gate plus a residual delta head that perturbs only the objects the gate flags) is a more effective and interpretable bias for physical prediction and control. On a MuJoCo tabletop pushing benchmark scaling from 3 to 8 objects, the sparse/residual model predicts next-state poses 2.5 to 4.6 times more accurately than a dense multilayer perceptron at 8.6 to 11.1 times fewer parameters, sustains change-detection F1 of 0.80 to 0.87 where the dense baseline is degenerate, transfers across object counts with zero retraining (99.4 percent F1 retention), and reaches about 90 percent of its full-data accuracy with a quarter of the data. In autoregressive rollout it compounds far less error, hugging the no-motion floor while the dense model drifts. Finally, inside a sampling-based planner, prediction-only models fail (though a true-simulator oracle solves the task with the identical planner, confirming the planner is sound), but once featurized and trained for the states a planner visits, the sparse model begins to plan (0.23 plus or minus 0.06 success over three seeds) while the dense monolith stays at zero at every seed. Modeling what changes, rather than re-predicting the whole world, is a simple, effective bias for object-centric physical AI; code, data generators, and all checkpoints will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [188] [Koopman-Based Robust Model Predictive Control for Nonlinear Systems with Stochastic Intermittent Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02079)
*Guanhua Liu,Tong Wu,Lixian Zhang,Weifeng Du,Minghao Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Koopman算子的随机模型预测控制（MPC）框架，能有效应对间歇式测量对受约束非线性系统控制带来的不确定性，并证明了闭环系统的有界性和递归可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPC在面对稀疏或间歇性的状态测量时，由于预测误差增大和状态复位的干扰，容易失去稳定性和可行性，因此需要一个能够兼容测量中断和自适应约束收缩的新方法。

Method: 作者提出用带Lipschitz约束的深Koopman模型建立线性隐空间预测器，将间歇测量建模为两模态离散时间马尔可夫链，构建一个统一的跳变误差模型。通过分布无关的概率性误差半径实现约束收缩，并用精确惩罚软约束机制处理复位跳跃和长时间中断。

Result: 在可验证条件下，预测误差均方最终有界且获得了二阶矩的明确上界。仿真结果表明该框架在视觉伺服跟踪任务中，在测量不确定性下仍保持良好控制性能，验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 此Koopman-MPC方法在处理测量间断条件下，实现了递归可行性和闭环误差有界性，是控制受约束非线性系统的重要进展，对实际带测量损失场景具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Intermittent state measurements pose fundamental challenges to model predictive control of constrained nonlinear systems because prediction uncertainty grows during feedback outages and measurement-triggered resets disrupt nominal state propagation, potentially compromising closed-loop stability and recursive feasibility. This paper develops a Koopman-based stochastic MPC framework with probabilistically truncated soft constraints. Specifically, a Lipschitz-constrained deep Koopman model provides a linear latent predictor, enabling computationally efficient online optimization. The intermittent measurement process is modeled as a two-mode discrete-time Markov chain, yielding a unified Markov jump error model for open-loop propagation and measurement-triggered resets. Under numerically verifiable sufficient conditions, the prediction error is shown to be mean-square ultimately bounded, and an explicit uniform second-moment bound is obtained. A distribution-free probabilistic error radius is then constructed for a prescribed confidence level and used to truncate dropout-dependent constraint tightening. An exact-penalty soft-constraint mechanism accommodates reset-induced jumps and prolonged dropouts. Under the stated terminal compatibility and bounded-disturbance conditions, recursive feasibility and mean-square ultimate boundedness of the closed-loop regulation error are established. Numerical simulations on a visual-servoing tracking task corroborate these theoretical results and demonstrate effective tracking under stochastic measurement unavailability.

</details>


### [189] [Unified Motion Retargeting for Humanoids with Learned Point Cloud Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02134)
*Hanyang Cao,Yuetong Fang,Taesoo Kwon,Runyi Yu,Ji Ma,Jing Tan,Yangchen Zhou,Baoze Du,Yi Gu,Yukang Gao,Ruoli Dai,Lei Han,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需手工设计语义映射的人形机器人动作迁移方法（UMR），通过点云学习实现高保真、可扩展的人体动作到机器人动作的转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作迁移方法主要依赖于手工设计的人-机关键点或身体对应关系，难以扩展到多样化的动作数据和不同结构的机器人，也难以还原细致的动作和交互细节。

Method: 提出Unified Motion Retargeting（UMR）框架，将人体与机器人的外部点云视为统一接口，通过学习稠密点云对应关系，无需手工映射即可实现动作迁移，并通过点云约束优化实现更精细的姿态对齐和交互动作传递。

Result: UMR能够统一地处理不同动作来源、不同机器人结构及多种场景（如行走和交互），实验结果表明UMR比现有方法具有更高的动作真实性和可行性。

Conclusion: UMR为大规模人体动作参考数据转化为机器人训练数据提供了可扩展、高质量的基础，可有效推动人形机器人学习的发展。

Abstract: Humanoid learning increasingly relies on transforming vast and diverse human motion data into high-quality robot reference trajectories. However, retargeting human motion to humanoid robots is challenging due to substantial differences in morphology, degrees of freedom, joint ranges, and kinematic constraints between humans and robots. Existing retargeting methods typically address these differences by defining human-robot correspondence through hand-crafted sparse keypoints or body-part pairs. As a result, retargeting quality depends heavily on manual semantic design, limiting scalability across motion sources and robot morphologies and providing only sparse guidance for reproducing detailed poses and interactions. In this paper, we present Unified Motion Retargeting (UMR), a framework that learns dense point cloud correspondence without requiring manually designed human-robot mappings. By treating exterior point clouds as a unified interface between human motion and humanoid robots, UMR decouples retargeting from source-specific skeletal semantics and robot-specific topology. The learned dense correspondence provides fine-grained geometric anchors for constrained point cloud matching optimization, enabling surface-level pose alignment and direct transfer of interaction contacts. Experiments demonstrate that UMR unifies retargeting across heterogeneous motion sources, robot embodiments, and downstream scenarios ranging from locomotion to interaction, while achieving higher motion fidelity and plausibility than state-of-the-art methods. UMR therefore provides a scalable foundation for transforming large-scale human motion references into robot-ready training data.

</details>


### [190] [Towards Effective Physical Reservoir Computing with a Pneumatic Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02157)
*Jeevan Hebbal Manjunath,Jun Wang,Suyi Li,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用气动软体臂作为物理储备计算平台，系统研究了气囊互连拓扑、机器人刚度及传感器数量对弯曲角估计性能的影响，提出了PRC软体机器人设计的三条实证准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储备计算在软体机器人状态估计与控制方面具有潜力，但目前缺乏关于软体 PRC 系统硬件设计有效性的系统研究和设计规范。

Method: 作者采用带有五囊感测柱的气动软体臂，系统比较了不同囊体拓扑结构、刚度和传感器数量，通过36组匹配实验，控同变量，在标准的0.2秒压力历史以及固定回归算法下，评测弯曲角估计性能。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 独立密封式囊体保持了更丰富的可观测状态；(2) 提高基线压力导致囊体响应冗余、尤其在联合拓扑下显著恶化估计精度；(3) 在密封拓扑下，两至三个精心布置的传感器即可恢复几乎全部性能，增加更多传感器收益极小。

Conclusion: 拓扑结构、刚度与传感器数量应协同优化设计，以提升软体机器人PRC的状态估计准确性，仅靠增强激励无法弥补设计不良带来的信息损失。

Abstract: Physical reservoir computing (PRC) refers to the use of a physical dynamical system as a computational resource for tasks such as state estimation and control, but there has been a lack of formal study of design rules towards more effective design of such physical reservoirs. Using a pneumatic soft arm with a five-pouch sensing column, this work studies how the pouch interconnection topology, robot stiffness, and the number of instrumented sensors affect bending-angle estimation performance. Across 36 matched trials spanning waveform, baseline pressure of the sensing column, and actuation range, all designs are evaluated under the same-time bending-angle estimation benchmark using 0.2 s of pressure history and a fixed ridge estimator. Our analysis of the experimental results leads to three design guidelines. First, independently sealed pouches preserve a much richer observable state than a shared manifold. Second, increasing the baseline pressure of the sensing column makes the pouch responses more redundant and increases estimation error most strongly in the coupled topology. Third, in the sealed topology, two strategically placed sensors already recover most of the attainable benefit, three capture essentially all of it, and additional sensors provide little or no additional value. In summary, the results suggest that topology, stiffness, and number of instrumented sensors should be co-designed for accurate PRC of soft robot states; stronger excitation alone cannot recover the diversity that poor design choices have already removed.

</details>


### [191] [Hardware-Accelerated Instance Segmentation for Resource-Constrained Space Robotics with Criticality Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02219)
*Siddhant Shete,Hilmi Dogu Kücüker,Udo Frese,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种适用于月球机器人、可在受限算力与恶劣环境下可靠运行的实例分割感知系统，兼顾量化校准与抗辐射故障处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球任务对自主机器人感知提出了高要求：极低光照、有限计算资源及辐射导致的硬件故障易损推理可靠性。现有方法难以兼顾高效、抗故障和高精度，需专门针对空间环境优化感知系统。

Method: 1. 提出激活方差信息采样（AVIS），这是一种无需标签、基于激活方差统计的量化校准样本选择方法。2. 对YOLO实例分割模型进行结构设计优化，使其部署于深度学习加速硬件（DPU）上，降低CPU依赖，并保障在低光照下有界延迟，静态编译运行。3. 引入关键性分析软件估算系统暴露在辐射故障下的风险，并据此指导相关缓解措施。

Result: 在月面微型机器人平台上，AVIS结合偏置校正后，可恢复69.8%的量化精度损失；推理延迟为309毫秒，功耗5.7瓦。定向故障缓解技术能减少31.7%的系统整体关键度，提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一套在空间部署约束下，集成量化校准与故障缓解的深度学习感知框架，为空间机器人AI感知系统的可靠、安全部署提供了可行蓝图。

Abstract: Autonomous lunar missions require real-time per- ception under three coupled constraints: extreme low-light conditions, limited onboard compute, and radiation-induced hardware faults that can silently corrupt inference. We present a deployment-oriented instance segmentation framework for resource-constrained lunar robotics that jointly addresses quan- tization calibration and system-level fault exposure under strict compute constraints. First, we introduce Activation Variance Informative Sampling (AVIS), a label-free calibration strategy that deterministically selects calibration samples based on activation variance statistics. Second, we deploy a YOLO-based segmentation model on a Deep Learning Processor Unit (DPU) with architectural modifications that reduce CPU fallback paths and enable statically compiled execution with bounded latency in low-lighting conditions. We further introduce a software-level criticality analysis to estimate fault exposure and guide mitigation under radiation-constrained operation. On a lunar micro-rover platform, AVIS with bias correction recovers 69.8% of quantization-induced accuracy loss while achieving 309 ms inference latency and 5.7 W power consumption. Targeted mitigation reduces global criticality by 31.7%. The results demonstrate an integrated approach and a blueprint for a reliable and safe AI perception framework under space deployment constraints.

</details>


### [192] [FOCUS: Foot Observation Confidence for Robust Humanoid Proprioceptive Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02222)
*Kaixin Feng,Angsong Li,Shaopeng Zhang,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,You Li,Haiyu Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于腿式机器人自主定位的连续足部运动学可靠性评估方法FOCUS，可大幅提高动态步态下的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的接触辅助定位方法主要依赖二值化的足部接触判断来决定是否信任前向运动学（FK）提供的速度观测，但在实际动态运动中部分支撑、脚尖拖拽、滑动等情况常导致单纯二值化判断失效，容易造成累积误差。

Method: 提出FOCUS框架：利用未标注仿真数据训练神经网络，输出每只脚连续的FK可靠性权重，将该权重用于自适应融合FK和IMU速度观测，并动态调整扩展卡尔曼滤波器(EKF)的观测协方差，无需人工标注连续FK可靠性。训练时采用FK加权的速度一致性损失和轻量仿真接触正则化。

Result: 在仿真走路实验中，FOCUS将绝对轨迹误差(ATE)降低83.7%；在真实19段行走中，ATE降低70.8%；在4种真实动态动作中，平均ATE降低42.7%。保持了动作尺度与光谱能量的真实性。

Conclusion: FOCUS无需复杂传感，自适应地融合自运动学和IMU信息，显著提升了动态腿式机器人定位的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Foot forward kinematics (FK) is widely used to improve proprioceptive legged odometry by providing reliable velocity constraints during foot support. Existing contact-aided estimators generally rely on binary contact decisions to determine whether the FK measurements of an entire foot should be trusted. However, contact does not necessarily imply FK reliability. Dynamic locomotion often involves partial support, toe dragging, and foot slip, causing binary contact decisions to accumulate significant drift over long trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose FOCUS (Foot Observation Confidence from Unannotated Simulation), which predicts a continuous FK reliability weight for each foot instead of estimating binary foot contact. Rather than replacing the model-based estimator, the predicted reliability weights are used to blend FK velocity observations with IMU-propagated body velocity and to adapt the observation covariance of an extended Kalman filter (EKF), enabling smooth reliability-aware fusion without hard contact switching. The network is trained from automatically generated simulation signals using an FK-weighted velocity consistency loss with lightweight simulator-contact regularization, without manually annotated continuous FK-reliability labels. The deployed model relies only on IMU and joint kinematic measurements, making it suitable for hardware platforms with unreliable torque sensing. Experiments demonstrate that FOCUS reduces absolute trajectory error (ATE) by 83.7% on simulated walking episodes, preserves simulated dynamic-motion fidelity in motion scale and spectral energy, reduces ATE by 70.8% across 19 real walking segments, and reduces mean ATE by 42.7% across four real dynamic-motion routines.

</details>


### [193] [DiffuSearch: How Hybrid Trajectory Planning Benefits from Aligned Objectives in Diffusion and Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02252)
*Steffen Hagedorn,Aron Distelzweig,Alexandru P. Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了DiffuSearch，一种结合扩散模型和蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的自主驾驶轨迹混合规划方法，实现了从轨迹生成到优化的统一目标，大幅提升了轨迹一致性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主驾驶轨迹混合规划架构往往由多个目标不一致的独立模块组成，导致轨迹初步生成和后续优化之间不一致，进而带来亚最优表现乃至安全隐患。作者希望通过统一的目标体系解决这一问题，提升整体轨迹规划性能。

Method: DiffuSearch方法采用两阶段架构，第一阶段利用带可微目标引导的扩散模型生成与场景一致的初始联合轨迹；第二阶段在离散动作空间下，利用与前述一致的目标作为奖励函数的MCTS对初始轨迹进行显式本地优化，实现解释性强且满足约束的精细调整。两阶段使用同一套目标，包括避碰、可行驶区域约束、舒适性和进度。

Result: 在nuPlan和interPlan闭环基准测试集上，DiffuSearch表现优异，多项指标达到或超过现有最佳水平，尤其在复杂交互场景下显著降低碰撞率、提升乘坐舒适性。消融实验显示MCTS优化为性能提升主要来源，而目标一致性带来更进一步改进。

Conclusion: DiffuSearch通过统一目标将扩散生成和MCTS优化有效结合，提升了轨迹规划的安全性、一致性和性能，为更可靠的无人驾驶规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: In trajectory planning for autonomous driving, hybrid planning architectures are often realized as a collection of disparate modules, each with its own objectives. This lack of a unifying principle can lead to inconsistencies between the initial and refined trajectory, resulting in suboptimal behavior. We address this by introducing DiffuSearch, a novel hybrid planner that uses a unified set of objectives across generation and refinement. Our model encourages all components to follow the same shared driving goals: collision avoidance, drivable area compliance, comfort, and progress. DiffuSearch employs a two-stage architecture. First, a guided diffusion model generates a scene-consistent, joint trajectory prediction, using our driving objectives as differentiable guidance functions to implicitly steer the denoising process. Second, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) in a discretized action space performs an explicit, local refinement of this proposal, leveraging the same driving objectives as its reward function. This synergistic design leverages the diffusion model's strength in finding scene-consistent solutions combined with the explainable, constraint-aware refinement of MCTS. Experiments on nuPlan and interPlan reactive closed-loop benchmarks demonstrate that DiffuSearch achieves strong and often state-of-the-art performance, substantially reducing collisions and improving comfort, particularly in complex, interactive scenarios. Our ablation studies indicate that MCTS refinement is the main mechanism behind the gains, while sharing objectives between implicit guidance and explicit search provides further consistent improvements.

</details>


### [194] [CrashDiffuser: VLM-Guided Collision Intent Reasoning for Fine-Grained Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02270)
*Shucheng Zhang,Yuang Zhang,Bingzhang Wang,Muhammad Monjurul Karim,Kehua Chen,Yinhai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CrashDiffuser的闭环扩散框架，用于生成目标碰撞部位可控的自动驾驶安全关键场景。其性能在多次尝试下显著优于现有方法，支持细粒度碰撞控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全场景生成主要关注碰撞本身，难以控制碰撞发生在目标车辆的特定部位（如前部、后部或侧面），缺乏细粒度测试手段，限制了对自动驾驶系统更细致的安全评估。

Method: CrashDiffuser结合可视语言模型（VLM）和扩散模型，先用VLM提取场景语义和结构化动作意图，再用扩散模型生成细粒度调整轨迹，并通过碰撞引导、候选选择和短时再规划动态适应目标车辆行为变化，实现碰撞位置的可控生成。

Result: 在WOMD闭环场景测试中，CrashDiffuser一次性生成目标碰撞的成功率为50.33%，三次尝试后的成功率提升到67.98%，指定碰撞部位的控制成功率为40.05%，同时生成的轨迹自然度具有竞争力。

Conclusion: CrashDiffuser有效提升了安全场景生成的精细度和可控性，为自动驾驶系统的测试与评估提供了更有针对性、高效的工具，组件消融分析也验证了方法设计的合理性。

Abstract: Generating safety-critical scenarios is essential for evaluating autonomous driving systems. However, existing generators primarily focus on inducing collisions and offer limited control over where contact occurs on the target vehicle. In this paper, we study fine-grained safety-critical scenario generation, where success requires both a target collision and a specified head, rear, or side contact region. We propose CrashDiffuser, a closed-loop VLM-guided diffusion framework that decouples semantic collision reasoning from continuous trajectory synthesis through a hierarchical collision-intent interface derived from the requested target contact region. At initialization, the VLM extracts reusable scene-level context; at each replanning step, it predicts a structured action tuple describing speed change, turning behavior, and collision stage. This intent conditions a diffusion model to generate executable adversarial trajectories, while collision-guided sampling, candidate selection, and short-horizon replanning adapt generation to the target vehicle's evolving behavior. On WOMD-derived closed-loop scenarios, CrashDiffuser achieves a target-collision rate of 50.33% in a single attempt and 67.98% after three attempts, together with a contact-region control success rate of 40.05% and competitive trajectory naturalness. Component ablations further support the proposed design.

</details>


### [195] [Contact-Constrained Lower-Limb Joint-Offset Calibration for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02306)
*Kaixiang Lu,Haiyu Lan,Chunxiao Qiao,You Li,Chengyuan Luo,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需外部设备、仅依赖本体传感器即可实现的人形机器人下肢关节编码器偏差自校准方法，提升了下肢运动学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人关节偏差标定方法多依赖外部设备（如光学动作捕捉系统或标志点），成本高且实用性较低。因此，开发无需外部辅助、仅靠机器人本体传感器实现高精度自标定方法，具备实际意义。

Method: 利用机器人双足静止接地时的运动学约束，仅用关节编码器及骨盆惯性测量单元(IMU)数据，通过最小化双脚间前向运动学变换的姿态相关散度，将偏差标定问题转化为12维非线性最小二乘优化，并结合海森矩阵特征结构分析关键可观方向，用合理的姿态激励与先验组合分离各关节偏差。

Result: 在仿真及实际机器人注入偏差后，该方法均能稳定恢复原始偏差。实际数据测试显示A3、A2平台脚高均方根残差分别从4.26降至2.20mm、8.03降至1.43mm。利用独立LiDAR-IMU基准验证pitch通道效果明显，去除注入的pitch偏差后，腿部里程计的垂直漂移回归激光轨迹。

Conclusion: 少量静止双足支撑数据即可为主要方向提供接触一致的校准修正，弱pitch链中各分量依赖姿态先验，方法在实际应用中展现出高效、实用的自标定能力。

Abstract: Accurate joint encoder offsets are essential for kinematic consistency in humanoid lower limbs, yet existing calibration methods typically require external motion-capture systems or fiducial targets. We present a self-contained calibration framework exploiting only onboard joint encoders and a pelvis-mounted IMU during static double-support contact. The inter-foot transform from forward kinematics must stay constant when both feet are fixed; minimizing its posture-dependent dispersion yields a nonlinear least-squares problem over the 12-dimensional offset vector. A Hessian eigenstructure analysis shows that parallel pitch axes induce a rotational coupling. Orientation residuals then observe only the pitch-offset sum, while translation and posture diversity set the remaining numerical observability. For the A3 pitch-to-roll-to-yaw ordering, hip-roll and hip-yaw excitation reduce hip-pitch coupling. A standing-posture knee prior then anchors the remaining weak pitch-chain decomposition. Simulation and real-machine injection tests show consistent recovery, and on held-out recordings calibration reduces foot-height RMS residuals from 4.26 to 2.20 mm on A3 and from 8.03 to 1.43 mm on A2. An independent LiDAR-inertial reference checks the pitch-coupled channel. Removing an injected pitch offset moves the leg-odometry vertical drift back toward the LiDAR trajectory. A few static double-support stances thus provide contact-consistent corrections for well-excited directions. Individual offsets in the weak pitch chain remain prior-dependent.

</details>


### [196] [From Multi-Fisheye Sensing to Panoramic Perception: A Parallax-Aware Onboard Platform for Ultra-Low-Altitude UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02319)
*Dun Dai,Ze Lu,Cheng He,Yaowen Wang,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套适用于超低空无人机的全景感知系统，通过结合多目鱼眼摄像头和先进嵌入式处理单元，实现了高效、高精度的实时全景视频拼接，并已在野外大规模测试，准确性和效率均达国际领先水平，核心代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低空无人机在建筑物、植被等障碍物环境中需要全方位视觉感知，传统全景拼接在视差处理和实时性上存在挑战。因此，需要开发一套具备视差感知能力并能嵌入无人机实时运行的全景感知系统。

Method: 设计了一套碳纤维无人机平台，集成了四目鱼眼摄像头、Jetson Orin NX嵌入式平台、飞控与GNSS。提出基于内容自适应接缝搜索、可验证残差网格、光度融合等的拼接算法，实现高精度与实时性兼顾。系统分别有精度优化和部署优化两个profile以适应不同应用场景。

Result: 用18组野外场景超5万组四视角数据充分评估。精度profile相比固定深度减少41.6%的远场特征错位；部署profile在不同测试场景下几何误差最小。20Hz重放下能以13.29W功耗持续输出19.99帧/秒。八分区ERP采样在视觉定位任务Recall@5达90.8%。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了视差感知、嵌入式实时执行及可复用下游接口一体化的全景感知方案，为超低空无人机等移动机器人在复杂环境下的视觉感知奠定基础。项目已开源，具有应用和研究价值。

Abstract: Ultra-low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) require surround vision near buildings, vegetation, and other obstacles. We present a parallax-aware onboard platform that converts four synchronized fisheye streams into an open 1280x640 equirectangular panorama (ERP) interface. A purpose-built carbon-fiber airframe integrates the cameras, NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX, a flight controller, and a global navigation satellite system (GNSS) receiver. The formation pipeline selects projection depth per overlap and combines controlled seams and photometric fusion. Its accuracy profile adds content-adaptive seam search and a validation-gated residual mesh, whereas its deployed profile retains margin-gated Per-seam updates for sensor-rate operation. Evaluation uses more than 50,000 four-view groups from 18 field sequences. Relative to Fixed Depth, the accuracy profile reduces far-field P90 feature misalignment by 41.6%; the deployed Per-seam profile achieves the lowest aggregate geometric errors across held-out sites. Under a paced 20 Hz replay, the deployed profile sustains 19.99 frames/s at 13.29W mean module-input power. Eight-sector ERP sampling reaches 90.8% mean daytime visual-place-recognition Recall@5. Together, these results validate an integrated onboard panoramic-perception architecture that unifies parallax-aware formation, sensor-rate embedded execution, and reusable downstream vision interfaces for ultra-low-altitude UAVs. The project has been open-sourced at https://github.com/DUNDAI1998/parallax-aware-uav-panorama.

</details>


### [197] [Humanoid Safe Stop via Learned Stoppability Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02358)
*Junfeng Long,Pieter Abbeel,Koushil Sreenath,Roberto Horowitz,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种名为Safe-Stop的新型紧急停止框架，通过联合两个可学习的停止可行性评估器，提高了仿人机器人在接到紧急停止指令时的安全性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统仿人机器人接到紧急停止命令时仅执行固定动作，未考虑当前状态是否真的可以安全停止，容易导致危险或性能损失。因此，需要更加智能、安全的停止决策机制。

Method: 将紧急停止问题表述为一个reach-avoid问题，提出Safe-Stop框架。该框架包括：1）一个训练得到的停止策略；2）两个互补的可学习评估器——停止概率评估器（通过实际停止结果监督）和基于Hamilton-Jacobi备份的可恢复性评估器。决策时，只有当两个评估器均判断能够安全停止时才执行停止，否则转为防跌倒策略。该方案对上游任务无关，具有良好的泛化性。

Result: Safe-Stop在机器人停止决策时能够做出更鲁棒且反应迅速的判断，实现了跨不同任务环境的迁移，无需针对新任务重新训练，并在安全性和灵活性之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: Safe-Stop框架有效提升了仿人机器人在处理紧急停止指令时的安全性、泛化性和决策鲁棒性。该方法为安全智能机器人的控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Humanoid robots responding to emergency stop commands typically execute a fixed maneuver, without reasoning about whether a safe stop is actually feasible from the current state. We cast emergency stopping as a reach-avoid problem and propose Safe-Stop, a task-agnostic framework that pairs a learned stop policy with learned stoppability estimators. The estimators are complementary: a stop-probability estimator supervised by the actual outcomes of the fixed stop policy, and a reach-avoidance estimator supervised by a Hamilton-Jacobi backup over physical state. The first captures emergent stopping behavior of the learned controller; the second provides a complementary recoverability signal. Because the stop policy and estimators do not depend on the behavior policy that preceded the stop command, they transfer across diverse upstream tasks without retraining. At deployment, the two estimates are combined: Safe-Stop commits to the stop only when both estimators indicate that stopping remains feasible, otherwise it hands off to a fall policy, instantiated as a damping fallback. This agreement check yields decisions that are robust without sacrificing reactivity.

</details>


### [198] [A Physics-Consistent Benchmark for Contact-Rich Human-Robot Interaction in Assistive Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02402)
*Chengxiao He,Shanghai Yuan,Liuqun Fan,Shenzhen Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一个用于评估人与机器人高接触协作任务（如机器人协助洗浴）的物理一致性基准体系，弥补以往只关注任务完成率而忽视物理互动质量的问题。实验对比分析了多种方法在物理和安全筛查前后的表现，发现仅以任务完成为指标并不足以评估人机接触的物理有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器人任务完成率评价标准无法反映人与机器人物理接触过程中可能出现的问题，尤其在需要逼真物理响应的辅助任务中。这种失效可能会导致实际部署时机器人带来安全隐患。因此，急需一种既能反映完成情况又兼顾物理互动安全性的评估体系。

Method: 作者提出了一个针对高接触人机交互（以机器人洗浴为例）的基准测试体系。该体系包括：1）采用可变形、被动响应的人体模型；2）结合物理感知得分和任务成功两大指标；3）采用只看摄像头和分开打分的评测协议；4）通过机器人对医疗假人的力-压入实验对仿真物理响应进行校准，确保评测的物理有效性。

Result: 在140次实验中，LLM增强状态机方法在只看任务完成时成功率为72.9%，引入区域和安全筛查后降至56.4%；另一算法VoxPoser接触更轻更稳定，但只完成了27.9%的任务；零-shot方法几乎无效。整体表明，任务完成率远不能说明物理接触的安全性和有效性。

Conclusion: 仅以任务成功率评价人机协作是不充分的，物理一致性和安全性评测尤为重要。本文提出的基准体系能够补齐这一空白，为辅助类机器人安全部署提供物理可行性评估工具。

Abstract: Conventional task-level evaluation asks whether a robot policy completes a specified action, but can miss failures that emerge only during physical human contact. This limitation is critical in contact-rich assistive tasks, where meaningful evaluation requires a physically responsive human, interaction-quality assessment beyond task success, and a leak-free observer-scorer protocol. We introduce a physics-consistent benchmark for contact-rich human-robot interaction, instantiated in robot-assisted bathing. The benchmark combines a deformable, passively responding human, physics-aware scores alongside task-level success, and a frozen vision-only / scorer-only evaluation protocol. To establish physical validity, region-wise simulated responses are calibrated against force-indentation measurements from Franka impedance pushes on a medical-care manikin. Under a frozen T1-T7 protocol with 140 runs per method, an LLM-augmented state machine (State Machine) achieves 72.9% task success but drops to 56.4% after correct-region and force-safety screening; VoxPoser produces lighter and more stable contact but completes only 27.9% of trials; and zero-shot pi0.5 achieves 0.7% task success with no correct-region or safety-gated successes.
