These results show that task completion alone does not imply physically valid contact and motivate physics-aware screening before deployment of contact-rich assistive robot policies.

</details>


### [199] [An Adaptive Control Architecture for Slope and Terrain Compensation in Autonomous Navigation in Mediterranean Greenhouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02487)
*Fernando Cañadas-Aránega,Dirk Wollherr,José L. Guzmán,José C. Moreno,José L. Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于温室等复杂环境的农业机器人地形自适应策略，通过实地数据和惯性测量装置（IMU）测量地形，结合模型预测控制器（MPC）和PI控制器，实现对不同地势与载重的有效轨迹跟踪，提高了机器人的导航精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂动态环境（如温室）中，地形的细微起伏都会对农业机器人的导航带来较大误差，因此需要开发能适应不同地形和负载变化的轨迹跟踪策略以保证移动机器人的稳定和精确导航。

Method: 该方法首先通过实验证明了对温室常见土壤（松软、混凝土、压实沙、砾石）的特性，并用IMU直接测量地形坡度，从而估算坡度对电机输入的影响。随后设计了级联轨迹跟踪系统，外环采用模型预测控制（MPC），内环为PI控制，且集成增益调度的前馈自适应控制机制，实时调整以适应坡度和地形变化带来的干扰。

Result: 仿真结果表明，采用本方法的差速驱动机器人在误差指标和控制信号效率上均有明显提升，突出显示了所提策略的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的面向载重的地形自适应策略，能够显著提升移动农业机器人在复杂地形下的轨迹跟踪表现，对于实际温室自动化具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: The ability to move stably over terrain with varying slopes and textures is essential for mobile agricultural robots operating in complex and dynamic environments such as greenhouses, where small terrain irregularities can lead to significant navigation errors. This article presents a novel terrain-adaptation strategy based on the carried payload, ensuring accurate and robust trajectory tracking. The proposed approach is based on: (i) the experimental characterization of the most common types of greenhouse soil, concrete, compacted sand, and gravel, and (ii) the direct measurement of terrain slope using the IMU, in order to estimate the force with which this angle affects the motor input. Based on this information, a cascade trajectory-tracking scheme has been designed, consisting of a model-based predictive controller (MPC) in the outer loop and a PI controller in the inner loop. The system incorporates an adaptive feedforward control through gain scheduling approach, capable of adjusting to disturbances caused by variations in slope and terrain type. Simulation results demonstrate that the differential-drive robot achieves a significant improvement both in error indices and in control signal efficiency, highlighting the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [200] [MS-MEM: Multi-Skill Manipulation-Enhanced Mapping via Uncertainty- and Disturbance-Aware Action Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02493)
*Yitian Shi,Jesper Mücke,Nils Dengler,Sicong Pan,Rania Rayyes,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向服务机器人的多技能操作增强建图（MS-MEM）方法，能在狭小杂乱环境中通过主动视角选择、推拣和抓取等多种技能协同，提高场景建图准确性，减少干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前许多服务机器人任务依赖于在狭小且空间受限的环境中准确识别和抓取物体。但由于遮挡严重、操作空间有限且需避免扰乱场景，现有方法在这些条件下表现不佳。

Method: 该文提出MS-MEM框架，融合主动视角选择、物体推拣与抓取三种操作。框架基于‘证据信念估计’进行不确定性感知，并采用新颖的抓取不确定性建模方法。对于不同动作，利用共同的信息增益标准评估，并提出‘附带干扰约束’（CDC）抑制对已高置信度区域的过度扰动，使策略在降低建图不确定性的同时，控制对场景的扰乱。

Result: 实验证明，和仅用单一技能或不考虑干扰的基线方法相比，MS-MEM不仅提升了建图准确率，还能显著降低对场景的扰动，展示出多技能协同的优势。

Conclusion: 多技能协同的主动感知与操作策略能有效提升服务机器人在受限复杂环境下的场景感知能力，兼顾高建图精度和低场景扰乱，对真实应用有较好前景。

Abstract: Accurate scene understanding in confined, cluttered spaces such as shelves is essential for service robots, as many everyday tasks require them to locate and retrieve objects reliably. Yet, it remains challenging due to severe occlusions, restricted accessibility, and the need to avoid excessive scene changes. In this paper, we propose Multi-Skill Manipulation-Enhanced Mapping (MS-MEM), an evidential framework for uncertainty-aware mapping that integrates active viewpoint selection, object pushing, and grasping. MS-MEM combines scene-level metric-semantic evidential belief estimators with an uncertainty-aware grasp representation. This representation is learned using a novel full-evidential grasp estimator that models both grasp affordance and orientation uncertainty. In our framework, candidate perception and manipulation actions are evaluated within a unified action selection pipeline using a common information gain criterion. For manipulation actions, we further introduce a collateral disturbance constraint (CDC) that discourages excessive changes to confident regions of the scene belief. This enables MS-MEM to select actions that effectively reduce map uncertainty while limiting collateral scene changes. Experimental results show that, compared with single-skill and unconstrained baselines that ignore scene disturbance, MS-MEM achieves higher mapping accuracy while substantially reducing scene disturbance, highlighting the synergistic effects of active viewpoint selection, push, and grasp actions.

</details>


### [201] [World-Model-Augmented Visual Locomotion for Humanoids on Foothold-Constrained Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02542)
*Yuxi Liu,Lijun Han,Ziming Wang,Ao Zhang,Cong Yang,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法WM-LOCO，通过强化学习让机器人在极具挑战性的稀疏支撑地形(如跳石、裂缝和窄台阶)上行走，大大提高了其适应性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏、不连续或几何限制明显的地形上，机器人失足往往无法恢复，传统依赖即时可见地形的足部放置策略高失败率。因此，亟需一种能够结合预判与环境感知的信息引导决策的方法。

Method: 提出世界模型增强视觉步行方法（WM-LOCO），将循环世界模型与PPO策略联合训练，输入机器人自身感知和单帧深度图像，世界模型输出预测特征，无需显式足点标签，直接指导步行策略。

Result: 在仿真中，WM-LOCO在跳跃和跳石场景下完全超过基线方法，在楼梯上与基线成功率相当但步态更高效且躯干晃动更小。实际部署于Unitree G1仿人机器人同样表现优异，三种地形平均通过率达93.3%。

Conclusion: WM-LOCO方法显著提升了在极具挑战性地形上的行走鲁棒性和效率，为未来机器人复杂环境自适应行走奠定基础。

Abstract: Foothold-constrained terrain is characterized by sparse, discontinuous, or geometrically restricted feasible foot contacts, as encountered on stepping stones, across gaps, and on narrow stair treads. On such terrain, a single misstep often leaves little room to recover, so policies that base foot-placement decisions primarily on the immediately visible terrain are prone to failure. We ask whether a learned predictive summary of near-future observations and rewards can provide the anticipatory information required in such settings. We present World-Model-Augmented Visual Locomotion (WM-LOCO), which jointly trains a recurrent world model and a PPO policy. Conditioned on proprioception and a single onboard depth image, the world model produces a predictive recurrent feature that guides the policy, without explicit foothold labels. In simulation, WM-LOCO succeeds on gaps and stepping stones where a matched baseline fails completely, and matches the baseline's success rate on stairs while improving stride efficiency and reducing pelvis acceleration. We deploy the same policy onboard a physical Unitree G1 humanoid using onboard proprioception and a single depth stream; it traverses all three terrain classes with an average success rate of 93.3%.

</details>


### [202] [ZETA: A Controlled Study of Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment VLA Transfer for Tabletop Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02546)
*Mi Yan,Wenhao Zhang,Zhiqi Zhang,Yu Peng,Tangxinyu Wang,Lingfei Zhai,Jiayi Su,Shengliang Deng,Lin Peng,Yaowei Liu,Yuxing Chen,Zhiyuan Wei,Jilong Wang,Jiayi Chen,Jiangran Lyu,Zhizheng Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文关注于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人硬件变化时的零样本泛化能力，提出了严格与预训练暴露两种零样本转移定义，并构建了包含14种目标体的真实与仿真基准，系统分析了影响零样本跨体泛化的四个关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人硬件不断演化，以及任务特定的数据收集成本高昂，开发能有效泛化到未知体（embodiments）的VLA模型变得十分重要。然而，目前学界对这一问题缺乏统一定义与系统实验。因此，本文旨在填补该领域的研究空白。

Method: 作者首先区分出严格零样本转移（目标体在训练数据里完全缺失）与预训练暴露零样本转移（只在预训练阶段出现）。随后，提出包含跨14个未见目标体的控制性评测基准，系统分析状态-动作表征、预训练体多样性、辅助协同训练目标及目标体暴露对跨体泛化的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示：采用局部末端执行器（EEF）的状态-动作表征、多样化源体及辅助协同训练可分别提升跨体泛化性能约15、18、7个百分点。同时，仅在预训练阶段加入5%的目标体数据，可提升13.4个百分点，验证了两类零样本转移的本质区别。

Conclusion: 本文为评估与提升跨体VLA模型泛化性能（以双指夹持桌面操作为例）提供了实用指导，并为扩展到移动底盘、高自由度手以及长任务规划等更广泛场景奠定基础。

Abstract: Zero-shot generalization to unseen embodiments is important for generalizable vision-language-action (VLA) models as robot hardware evolves and task-specific data collection remains costly. However, a systematic understanding of this problem remains limited, in part because the literature lacks a unified zero-shot transfer definition and controlled evaluation settings that isolate embodiment changes from differences in tasks, scenes, or protocols. To address this gap, we first distinguish strict zero-shot transfer, where the target embodiment is absent from all training data, from pretrain-exposed zero-shot transfer, where it appears only during pretraining. We then introduce a controlled benchmark spanning 14 held-out target embodiments across simulation and real-world validation. Within this framework, we conduct a controlled analysis of four factors: state-action representations, pretraining embodiment diversity, auxiliary co-training objectives, and target-embodiment exposure. Experimental results show that local end-effector (EEF) state-action representations, the source embodiment diversity, and auxiliary co-training improve cross-embodiment transfer by around 15, 18, and 7 percentage points, respectively. We further find that adding only 5% target-embodiment data during pretraining improves average target-embodiment progress by 13.4 percentage points, showing that strict and pretrain-exposed zero-shot transfer are distinct and should be reported separately. Together, these findings provide practical guidance for evaluating and improving cross-embodiment VLA transfer in stationary tabletop manipulation with two-finger grippers, while motivating future investigation of broader settings including mobile-base control, dexterous hands, and long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [203] [Pre-Lane-change Signal in Transitional Autonomous Vehicles: Results from Controlled Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02575)
*Zeyu Mu,Danjue Chen,Abhinav Sharma,George F. List*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过150次自动驾驶车辆强制变道实验，研究了其在变道决策及执行过程中的纵向准备与目标间隙选择机制。提出模型准确预测最终并道方式，并发现变道分为纵向准备和横向执行两个阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在强制变道时，如何选择目标变道间隙以及纵向准备过程尚不清晰。厘清这一过程有助于提高自动驾驶安全与决策合理性。

Method: 作者基于NC-tALC实验中的150次强制变道数据，将‘信号时间’(SigT)定义为变道准备的起点，采用Firth逻辑回归，以SigT时刻的相对位置和速度预测车辆最终是在目标车道车辆前插入还是后并入，再分别分析处于最佳位置(in-position)与需调整(repositioning)两类车辆的纵向推进过程。

Result: 模型能准确预测车辆是前插还是后并入，平均五折交叉验证准确率达0.89。实验显示，不同的准备方式（in-position vs. repositioning）在纵向推进上表现出不同路径，并为两类情景提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 研究验证并细化了自动驾驶变道的‘两阶段’推断：从SigT到真正变道前的纵向准备，以及后续的横向操作执行。该模型为独立建模目标间隙选择与横向启动提供了理论支持，并适用于多种变道准备场景。

Abstract: This paper investigates how a production transitional autonomous vehicle (tAV) develops and executes mandatory lane-change decisions. Using 150 controlled mandatory lane changes from the NC-tALC experiments, the study examines whether the eventual target gap is observable before lateral movement begins and how the tAV progresses longitudinally from that pre-lane-change state to lane-change start.
  Signal time (SigT) is defined as an operational pre-lane-change-start reference point. A Firth logistic regression predicts whether the tAV eventually merges in front of or behind its nearest target-lane vehicle using relative position and relative speed at SigT. Longitudinal progression from SigT to lane-change start is then examined separately for in-position and repositioning cases.
  The traffic state at SigT contains substantial information about eventual target-gap choice and provides meaningful lead time before lateral movement begins. The proposed formulation predicts whether the tAV remains with its current gap or repositions to a neighboring gap by moving forward or dropping back, including cases with longitudinal overlap and ambiguous current-gap geometry. The model achieves an average five-fold cross-validated accuracy of 0.89. Results also provide preliminary evidence that in-position and repositioning cases follow different longitudinal pathways from SigT to lane-change start.
  These findings support a two-stage conjecture of the observable lane-change process: longitudinal preparation from SigT to lane-change start, followed by lateral maneuver execution. The formulation applies to in-position, repositioning, and longitudinally overlapping cases, and can support lane-change models that distinguish target-gap choice from lateral-onset timing while representing longitudinal preparation before lateral movement begins.

</details>


### [204] [Advancing Accessible Underwater Robotics: The Mini-Girona I-AUV at RAMI 2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02605)
*Taqi Hamoda,Bilal Ahmed,Deborah Ele-Ojo,Thi Tran Ha Bao,Adel Saidani,Mazen Elgabalawy,Alaaeddine Chaarani,Sebastian Realpe,Patryk Cieslak,Pere Ridao,Narcis Palomeras,Nuno Gracias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mini-Girona I-AUV是一款经济实用、面向教育和研究的自主水下机器人，配备先进的操作与感知能力，在学生竞赛中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高端水下机器人价格昂贵，难以普及，亟需兼具功能性和可负担性的机器人平台，助力研究和教育。

Method: 设计并开发了Mini-Girona I-AUV，结合5自由度机械臂、立体视觉系统和AI处理模块，实现自主导航与作业。在RAMI 2025学生竞赛中对其性能进行了测试。

Result: 虽然遇到热管理和团队操作受限的问题，Mini-Girona在感知与干预任务中表现优异，最终获得总分第二名。

Conclusion: Mini-Girona展示了其作为经济型水下自主机器人的应用潜力，有助于推动水下机器人技术在科研和教育领域的创新与普及。

Abstract: The Mini-Girona Intervention Autonomous Underwater Vehicle (I-AUV) represents an advancement in accessible underwater robotics, designed to bridge the gap between costly, specialized research AUVs and basic Remotely Operated Vehicles (ROVs). Developed with a focus on affordability and usability, the Mini-Girona, priced at approximately $50,000, integrates advanced components such as a 5-DOF manipulator arm, stereo vision, and AI-driven processing for autonomous navigation and intervention tasks. This paper presents the design and development of the Mini-Girona, detailing its performance during the RAMI 2025 student competition. Despite challenges such as thermal management issues and restricted team access, the Mini-Girona achieved second place overall, excelling in vision-based perception and intervention tasks. This work highlights the platform's potential as a tool for underwater robotics research and education, fostering innovation in real-world underwater applications.

</details>


### [205] [Latent Cluster Analysis for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02634)
*Theodor Wulff,Sergio Lanza,Tamara Bila,Angelo Cangelosi,Stefan Wermter,Igor Farkas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种分析视觉-语言-行动模型（VLA）内部表示的方法LAVLA，对主流模型GR00T N1.5进行了层级分析，并提出了新的聚类与可解释性提升方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前VLA模型越来越多应用于机器人领域，能够将语言与感知有效绑定到行动，但这些模型的内部表示和决策机制尚不清楚，缺乏可解释性分析工具。

Method: 提出了LAVLA框架，对VLA模型进行层级的潜在聚类分析，尤其关注其行动解码器。引入了基于交叉注意力的嵌入加权方法，突出有效特征并压制不相关特征。通过加权聚类与基线方法进行定量比较。用于提升可解释性的措施包括为每个聚类提取出人类可解释的语义描述。

Result: 定量评估显示加权后聚类效果优于基线。分析发现随着深度的增加，模型内部的潜在聚类逐步分离出时空和运动学特征，中间层的表征更精细，输出层趋于稳定。

Conclusion: LAVLA框架提升了机器人领域VLA模型的可解释性，帮助揭示其内部表征的逐步演化，为理解和改进这类模型提供新手段。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Models are increasingly used in robotics for their ability to ground language and perception into action, yet the internal representations driving their behaviour remain poorly understood. We propose LAVLA, a framework for latent cluster analysis of VLA models, and conduct a layer-wise study of the state-of-the-art GR00T N1.5 model, with particular focus on its action decoder. To better characterise the latent space during action diffusion, we introduce a cross-attention-based embedding-weighting method that amplifies relevant features while suppressing less informative ones. Quantitative evaluation shows that weighted clustering consistently outperforms the baseline. To improve interpretability, we extract human-interpretable concepts for each cluster, linking latent representations to semantic descriptions. Our analysis shows that latent clusters progressively disentangle spatiotemporal and kinematic features, with representations becoming more refined in the middle layers and stabilising toward the output. As such, LAVLA advances the interpretability of language-driven robotic systems.

</details>


### [206] [HINT: Human-Intent Inception for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02653)
*Mingyu Mei,Haojie Xu,Shihao Jin,Zibo Dai,Qihao Cheng,Zhengrui Lv,Hongjie Fang,Shirun Tang,Guang Chen,Xinyue Zhao,Huiliang Shen,Zaixing He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了HINT框架，通过模仿人类的操作原则，有效提升VLA模型在复杂场景下的意图理解与任务执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型难以在视觉密集、持续变化的环境中，仅通过稀疏的语言提示实现高水平的智能操作，容易被视觉相关性主导而忽略人类真实意图。

Method: 提出HINT（Human-INTent INcepTion）框架，在操作模式切换时才做语义推理，其余时刻依赖视觉跟踪和多视图定位维护当前任务意图，借助图像语义高亮和关注先验注入两种接口向动作策略传递意图，无需为基础策略引入新的可训练参数。

Result: 在三种长序列任务及其分布外变体上，HINT显著提升了动作策略对人类意图的理解、任务进展及成功率，并保持了实时的低延迟控制性能。

Conclusion: HINT框架能更好地模拟人类操作优势，为提升VLA模型的智能化和泛化能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Humans can perform complex manipulations given a simple intent through an overall instruction, while continuously adapting to evolving visual observations. However, current vision-language action (VLA) models and other action policies struggle to realize this high-level intelligent behavior under dense, evolving visual inputs and sparse language guidance. Visual correlations can then dominate semantic intent, leading actions to follow visual shortcuts rather than human goals. We present HINT (Human-INTent INcepTion), an agentic framework inspired by the human manipulation principles: semantic intent changes sparsely at manipulation-pattern transitions, whereas continuous control primarily depends on the evolving object-hand relationship. HINT invokes semantic reasoning only at pattern transitions to resolve the current subtask and target, then maintains this commitment through multi-view grounding and visual tracking. We explore two visual interfaces-image-space semantic highlighting and attention-prior injection-to communicate the tracked intent to the action policy without introducing additional trainable parameters into the foundation action model. Experiments across three long-horizon tasks and out-of-distribution variants show that HINT substantially improves intent understanding, task progress, and end-to-end success across two foundation policies while preserving low-latency control.

</details>


### [207] [From Proxy Learning to Driving Decisions: A Transfer-Based Framework for Evaluating Future-Aware Autonomous Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02688)
*Yikai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Proxy-to-Decision Transfer (PDT) 框架，用于分析代理目标改进在自动驾驶规划中的实际决策效用，并系统性地揭示代理指标与真实驾驶决策改进之间的关系与差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划工作常以代理目标（如未来预测准确性）改进证明算法有效性，但这些提升未必能真正转化为实际决策质量的提升。缺乏系统分析工具去验证代理目标改进何时确实带来真实决策层面的效益。

Method: 提出PDT分析框架，包括两个主要模块：1）决策转移分解模块，定位代理与决策目标之间的价值损失；2）可靠性约束验证模块，设计严苛的、完整的实验标准来验证改进是否在真实决策层面有效。

Result: 实验证明，即使部分代理目标（如BCE、PDM等）有所下降，在完整决策流程下未必能得到同样明显的收益。局部或子集上的微小提升在全局评估和更大规模条件下难以重复，严格的鲁棒性测试下全部测试配置均未通过。

Conclusion: PDT框架能够有效甄别代理目标提升无法可靠转化为真实自动驾驶决策改进的情形，为领域提供了更严谨的算法评估和可靠性检验工具。

Abstract: Future-aware representations and world models are increasingly used in proposal-based autonomous-driving planners to improve trajectory selection. However, improvements in proxy objectives or restricted subsets are often interpreted as planning gains without verifying proposal ordering, selected trajectories, full-scale utility, and critical driving components. We propose the Proxy-to-Decision Transfer (PDT) Framework, an analysis framework that evaluates when learned future information supports a reliable driving-performance improvement claim. Its Decision-Transfer Decomposition Module localizes value loss through score margins, switch-conditioned utility, and support-versus-selection regret. Its Reliability-Constrained Validation Module requires exact pairing, a minimum meaningful effect, scale-expanded confirmation, safety non-compensation, sequential comparability, and family-level robustness. On a representative future-aware planner evaluated with NAVSIM-v1, component BCE decreases from 0.705 to 0.530 while held selected PDM decreases from 0.963 to 0.961. A separate candidate improves a 512-record prefix by 0.00909, with a scene-bootstrap 95% interval of [0.000744, 0.0177], but its 2048-record and complete-support intervals include zero. A proposal-level replay further confirms the switch-utility decomposition, yet none of 432 screened configurations passes the two-half, two-seed robustness gate. PDT therefore identifies where decision transfer fails or remains indeterminate across proxy, subset, aggregate, and selection evidence.

</details>


### [208] [Do Better Imagined Rollouts Mean Better Robot Control? A Controlled Study of World-Model Evaluation Under Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02811)
*Dharini Raghavan,Amritpal Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对机器人中预测模型的评估方法进行了深入分析，发现当前常用的固定步长开环评估与实际闭环运行结果存在较大差异，并提出应结合预测时长与测量更新频率来更准确评估状态估计器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，预测模型常以开环（脱离实际控制反馈）下的短期准确率进行评判，但实际机器人任务（如路径跟踪）是在闭环系统中执行的，两者评价结果常常不一致。该研究旨在系统分析为何这种差异存在，以及如何改善评估流程，使选出的模型在现实应用中表现更佳。

Method: 作者在有偏航迹和间歇性地标观测条件下，设定了差动驱动小车的路径跟踪任务，设计了24种不同传感环境，比较了六种状态估计器，通过轨迹回放、固定步长（20步）开环滚动和实际闭环路径跟踪等三种方法计算和对比评估误差，分析了测量更新间隔和预测时长变化对结果的影响，还尝试训练了能适应长时间无观测的递归估计器。

Result: 实验发现，轨迹回放评估与闭环表现的相关性高于固定步长滚动，20步无观测情况下不同估计器的选择出现较大偏差；随着步长增加和测量更新减少，开环评估和闭环结果关联显著下降。同时，对于能够适应长时间无观测的递归估计器，在整体感知退化情况下表现有所提升，但提升具有不一致性。

Conclusion: 机器人预测模型的评估指标应明确预测步长和测量更新策略。在用于反馈控制场景下，脱离实际观测频率的开环评估可能误选模型，离线评估应模拟真实闭环的感知和校正节奏，才能更准确反映模型实际效能。

Abstract: Predictive models are increasingly used in robotics for state estimation, planning, control, and policy evaluation, yet they are often judged by open-loop prediction accuracy over a fixed horizon. In closed-loop operation, a robot repeatedly acts, receives new measurements, updates its state estimate, and recomputes control. We study this difference in a differential-drive path-tracking task with biased odometry and intermittent landmark sensing. Six state estimators are evaluated across 24 sensing conditions using trajectory replay, a 20-step measurement-free rollout, and closed-loop tracking. Replay position RMSE correlates more strongly with closed-loop cross-track RMSE than rollout error (Spearman rho = 0.923 vs. 0.774) and selects a different estimator from the closed-loop optimum in 5/24 conditions, compared with 18/24 for the rollout metric. We then vary rollout horizon and measurement-update interval. With H=20, rank agreement decreases from rho = 0.916 with measurements at every step to rho = 0.774 with no measurements. A horizon-update grid shows that long prediction horizons remain informative when regular corrections are retained, whereas long rollouts without correction can produce rankings that differ substantially from closed-loop behavior. We also test recurrent estimators trained on longer sensing outages. This improves the EKF-anchored models under combined sensing degradation, reducing GRU-EKF cross-track RMSE from 1.72 m to 1.06 m, but the gain is not consistent across isolated outages or estimator architectures. These results show that predictive-model evaluation in robotics should specify both prediction horizon and measurement-update schedule. For models used in feedback, offline rollouts are most informative when their sensing and correction pattern reflects closed-loop operation. Code is available at https://github.com/rdharini2001/Robot_World_Model

</details>


### [209] [Toward Robust LiDAR Semantic Segmentation for Real-World Deployment: Evaluation under Coarse Labels, Adverse Conditions, and Domain Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02830)
*Samir Abou Haidar,Alexandre Chariot,Mehdi Darouich,Cyril Joly,Jean-Emmanuel Deschaud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对现有LiDAR语义分割方法的评估局限，提出了一套更贴近实际部署需求的综合评估协议，从标签粒度、安全鲁棒性和跨域泛化三个方面系统评估模型部署准备度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LiDAR语义分割在标准基准测试上取得了很强的性能表现，但现有评测大多仅限于干净、单一场景和细粒度标签，未能全面评估部署时的安全语义、感知退化及跨域能力。实际应用中，系统需面对安全关键的标签分辨能力、有损读数环境以及跨数据集泛化需求，因此亟需一套统一并具备实际意义的评估协议。

Method: 作者提出了三维评估协议：（1）按安全相关的粗粒度标签进行模型评测，分析不同标签粒度对方法能力的影响；（2）在八类模拟实际环境中传感器退化的LiDAR畸变下，系统检测模型鲁棒性；（3）在无数据适配条件下，评估模型的跨域泛化能力。同时，还加入了Jetson AGX Orin嵌入式硬件上的推理速度评测，反映部署算力约束。

Result: 实验证明：现有模型在细粒度基准上的排名与实际安全表现不总是相关；所有方法在畸变环境下均有明显性能下降，并且各自的鲁棒性受模型架构影响；现有算法的跨域泛化能力远未达到可靠部署的要求。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了一系列基准表现与现实部署能力之间的实际差距，推动了LiDAR语义分割更为落地、科学的评估实践，并为后续研究提供了具备现实场景的参考协议。

Abstract: LiDAR-based semantic segmentation is a core perception module for autonomous vehicles and mobile robots. Despite the strong performance of recent state-of-the-art methods on standard benchmarks, existing evaluation protocols remain focused on clean, single-domain settings and fine-grained label taxonomies, leaving deployment readiness largely unassessed. Real-world systems must handle safety-critical label semantics, degraded sensing conditions, and cross-domain variability, yet no unified protocol currently addresses all three aspects together. In this paper, we propose a structured evaluation protocol that assesses the deployment readiness of LiDAR semantic segmentation models along three complementary dimensions: (i) coarse-label evaluation aligned with autonomous driving safety priorities, revealing how label granularity affects different methods; (ii) robustness under eight types of LiDAR corruptions designed to emulate real-world atmospheric, geometric, and sensor degradations; and (iii) domain generalization across datasets without adaptation. The evaluation includes inference speed measured on an embedded Jetson AGX Orin platform, directly reflecting deployment constraints. Our results show that fine-grained benchmark rankings do not always reflect safety-relevant performance, that all methods experience substantial degradation under corruptions with architecture-dependent robustness characteristics, and that current domain generalization remains insufficient for reliable deployment. These findings expose concrete gaps between benchmark performance and deployment readiness, and provide a reference protocol for more practically grounded evaluation of LiDAR semantic segmentation.

</details>


### [210] [Towards Trustworthy Autonomous Robots: An Explainable AI-Based Decision Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02861)
*Cagri Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了TRACE框架，实现了机器人决策全过程的可追溯与可审计。通过分层架构记录每一步决策背后的传感器证据、推理和执行，实验证明TRACE大幅提升了三项可审计性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习自主机器人在发生事件时，难以溯源具体决策原因。为满足监管与安全需求，必须提供决策审查与解释的有效机制。

Method: 设计了TRACE框架，包括语义感知、信念推理、动作合成和执行校验四个可审计层，并与感知模型无关，支持集成CNN/transformer等深度学习模块，同时保障决策链条的完整记录和回溯。

Result: 实验证明TRACE在仓库机器人导航任务中，证据可追溯性达98.6%，决策可回溯性99.0%，时间连续性98.1%，远超传统LIME等事后解释方法。

Conclusion: TRACE有效提升了高风险自主系统的决策可解释性与合规性，为AI安全可审计监管法案的技术落地与关键领域安全AI提供了新方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robots powered by deep learning face a fundamental auditability challenge: when incidents occur, investigators cannot reconstruct why the system made specific decisions. This paper presents TRACE (Transparent Reasoning Architecture for Credible Execution), a decision framework that ensures every autonomous action can be traced back to sensor evidence through documented causal chains. The framework organizes decision-making into four auditable layers: Semantic Perception for evidence-grounded entity recognition, Belief Reasoning for probabilistic state estimation with causal graphs, Action Synthesis for constraint-aware planning with counterfactual documentation, and Execution Verification for compliance monitoring. TRACE is model-agnostic yet designed to integrate learning-based perception modules (CNNs, transformers) while preserving decision-level auditability. We evaluate the framework using three objective metrics: Evidence Traceability (sensor-to-decision linkage), Decision Reconstructability (post-hoc analysis capability), and Temporal Continuity (audit trail completeness). Experimental evaluation on warehouse robot navigation demonstrates that TRACE achieves 98.6% evidence traceability, 99.0% temporal continuity, and 98.1% decision reconstructability across 500 simulated decision cycles. Post-hoc methods like LIME provide feature attributions but lack the artifact structure needed for decision-level reconstruction. The framework addresses EU AI Act requirements for high-risk system transparency and contributes to Explainable AI for safety-critical autonomous systems.

</details>
